[Matched model calls and provenance checks](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/README.md#put-the-decisive-quote-before-or-after-the-relation)

With the stronger reader and the same bound writer, the frozen router releases 34.35% actionable briefs on Bosnia and Afghanistan versus 20.93% when acknowledgment is erased. Retrained independent routing reaches 35.63%, and greedy reaches 37.30%. Greedy and learned are effectively tied after simulated fees. Include strong readers and independent retraining so weak classifiers and erased expected feedback cannot inflate the measured interaction benefit. [Final-record scoring and exact receipt replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-quote-order-evidence/results.json)

### Prove the routing ceiling and find indistinguishable cases

Enumerate the exact best local tables with and without acknowledgment using the same archived prose and simulated delivery. The adaptive ceiling is 0.251 on Bosnia and Afghanistan and 0.382 on Abyei and the Central African Republic. Greedy already earns 0.242 and 0.367. The UNIFIL and Colombia ceiling is 0.090 versus −0.037 for greedy. A new fit on synthetic 15% relation errors improves its synthetic objective from 0.115 to 0.117 and leaves measured final-record outcomes unchanged. [Exact solver and one synthetic-noise fit](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/README.md#measure-exact-routing-headroom-and-collisions-in-the-source-labels)

An identical observed pair of support and contradiction describes one false objection and three genuine ones. An exact policy tuned to eliminate the false revision suppresses all three valid revisions as well. It has no feature that separates those cases. Check what the source says about the target actor, act and scope before compressing it to a relation. Score whether that distinction changes the routing decision and the usable final brief on cases that have the same original labels. [Proven shared receipt probabilities and exact replays](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-receipt-alias-results.json)

### Check whether a witnessed objection entails a contradiction

Show the claim, exact quote and source to a compatibility checker for each reported contradiction. It says open in all 14 probes, so the runtime removes the false UNIFIL revision and also loses every valid revision. Only one of five routed explanations is sound. It correctly identifies the Colombian condition as incompatible with an unconditional call while printing open. The UNIFIL open label accompanies an invented attribution. [Prompts, exact reasons and replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/README.md#verify-that-an-apparent-contradiction-rules-out-the-exact-claim)

The authored scope check removes the UNIFIL false actions and retains 18.54% usable revisions on UNIFIL and Colombia. The model check produces zero revisions on every panel and exactly matches the all-suppressed control. Grade evidence scope, label–reason agreement and valid-revision recall independently. A decline in false actions alone can reward a gate that discards every genuine objection. [Separate outcomes by case and policy](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-scope-check-evidence/results.json)

### Preserve genuine objections with a bounded scope override

Compare the possible meanings of a same-actor coverage statement and check the vote polarity only on the same recorded resolution. A literal statement that coverage fell short of full is compatible with partial coverage. The scoped override fixes the erroneous UNIFIL objection and independently confirms the genuine Abyei abstention. It preserves the other three original objections outside its grammar. All 46 synthetic scope, polarity and attribution controls pass. [Grammar, controls and evaluated cases](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/README.md#check-covered-scope-and-recorded-votes-with-bounded-source-rules)

On split UNIFIL and Colombia, false revisions fall from 18.56% to zero and usable revisions remain at 18.54%. Frozen adaptive routing reaches 0.171 net against 0.161 for the exact independent ceiling and 0.160 for greedy. The exact adaptive ceiling is 0.176 with free acknowledgment. This shows a coordination advantage over independent sends on the corrected fixture. The bounded parser recognizes just two of the five routed objections and the original reader supplies the others. Test new source families with the same rules and evaluate the validity of unresolved forms separately. [Exact costs and receipt-level traces](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-scope-rule-results.json)

### Fresh ordinary-language meeting cards and the limits of exact quotes

I ran frozen Qwen3.5-4B on six proposals from the Gulf of Guinea piracy vote and the Libya mission renewal. The former joins UNBench author-test event 2438, and the latter uses an official meeting record. The pinned UNSC-Bench release contains neither of these two meetings under the audited identifiers. The local reader got 11/12 relations right. The error came from a real quote about the geographic reach of a resolution that left the reach of the underlying convention open. Full access masks the false revision because a different card gives a genuine contradiction. Include receipt masks that expose a single misleading witness alongside complete-packet scoring. [Sources and release joins](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/README.md#transfer-to-ordinary-source-summaries-on-the-gulf-of-guinea-and-libya)

The previous scope rule recognizes 0/6 literal contradictions on these ordinary paraphrases. Requiring rule verification drives frozen false actions from 7.34% to zero under split holdings and eliminates all usable revisions (from 22.02%). With literal evidence, frozen adaptive net is 0.093, greedy is 0.086, and the exact independent and adaptive ceilings are 0.083 and 0.096. Greedy delivers more usable revisions and incurs more false actions. Report both event-level errors and the cost-adjusted aggregate. [Receipt replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-new-meetings-evidence/results.json)

With all cards supplied, the compiled reader gets 6/6 dispositions right and writes 1/6 strict briefs. The full-access solo chair and independent-drafts synthesis each get 2/6 dispositions and 0/6 strict briefs. Their principal disposition error is calling decisive contrary evidence insufficient evidence. The local writer introduces its own failures, including turning observed votes into statements. All 36 generations replay with zero model updates. The prose review is same-assistant and the systems have unequal call budgets. Treat this as a documentary diagnostic. Use typed roll-call evidence as a control for vote questions, then test whether live peer clarification improves what the final record preserves under matched budgets. [Comparison protocol and results](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/README.md#transfer-to-ordinary-source-summaries-on-the-gulf-of-guinea-and-libya)

### Separate roll-call extraction, formatting, and final attribution

On the two exposed meeting families, substituting supplied official vote labels changes none of the vote dispositions. Rendering a sentence from each received actor–resolution–vote tuple improves strict vote briefs from 5/12 to 12/12 over nonempty receipt masks. Both fully supplied open-text briefs still fail the strict rubric. With the literal open-text reader, the frozen adaptive router reaches 0.315 net on split holdings, compared with 0.283 for greedy, 0.370 for exact independent and 0.402 for exact adaptive. Suppressing unverified open-text contradictions removes the false revision and also loses China's genuine objection in the China-only view. In that system, frozen reaches 0.334 and greedy 0.337. Neither variant gives a robust advantage for the frozen router over the strongest control. [Component results and exact solver](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/README.md#bind-recorded-acts-before-compiling-vote-briefs)

Across 32 new generations, separate the vote label, literal citation, and protocol format. The first prompt elicits the placeholder instead of source words on all four historical cards. The second elicits correct spans but with commas, so the original gate still admits zero historical votes. A post hoc comma-tolerant gate yields 4/4 correct historical statuses and 12/12 correct fictional statuses. Only 2/16 revised answers follow the original format across vote and unknown replies. The historical cards all contain yes votes. Use actual abstentions and no votes in a prospective split. Keep this typed control distinct from open-text deliberation and measure whether a peer's contribution changes the final record with the same evidence and call budget available to the controls. [Prompt contrasts and receipt replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-vote-channel-format-evidence/results.json)

### Actual votes on competing drafts and amendments

Use S/PV.9616 and S/PV.9453 to score the exact actor, document symbol, and vote group together. Four texts join UNBench, and two join pinned UNSC-Bench rows with all 30 votes agreeing. The two meetings yield four normalized roll-call blocks and 16 actor–text tests, with both combined and target-only views. Exact quote admission on the combined views got 10/16 right and admitted four wrong votes. Binding the quote to the actor, symbol, and label eliminated those errors. Target-only views got 11/16 and deterministic lookup got 16/16. The source already has structure, so the lookup is the strongest control for vote attribution. [Packet, join audit, and 32 generations](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/README.md#bind-votes-to-competing-drafts-and-amendments-in-the-same-meeting)

On eight authored claims and all four receipt masks, binding dropped false dispositions from three to zero. Split-holdings net for frozen routing rose from 0.151 to 0.335 using the same combined model outputs. With correct typed votes, net was 0.566 frozen, 0.569 acknowledgment-aware greedy, 0.531 exact independent and 0.572 exact adaptive. This isolates a role for delivery feedback and shows how little room the learned policy has to improve on a strong heuristic in this vote exercise. Keep these cases in the factual guard suite. Evaluate live peer turns on open-text disagreements and require a traceable improvement in the final record against matched solo and independent baselines. [Full replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-mixed-votes-evidence/results.json)

### Live peer turn with a matched independent-drafts control

Hold the same first note, answering desk card, final source packet, model, and output caps fixed. Let the answering desk see the first note and question in just the peer arm. Give a separate three-pass solo analyst both cards on all three calls. On two open-text proposals with both desk orders, peer briefs average 46/70, independent 40/70, and solo 35/70. Correct dispositions are 2/4, 2/4 and 4/4 respectively. All twelve briefs fail the strict contract. The peer treatment earns 12 more points in two pairs and ties the other two. It preserves the Ghana evidence gap once and avoids one unsupported historical claim about the UK. Reply units are the same in peer and independent on all four panels, and only two shared openings ask an explicit or indirect question. Track preservation of shared reply units and distinctions between missing evidence and contrary evidence, alongside genuinely new contributions. Save exact spans from the opening, reply and final. This is a same-assistant diagnostic on two exposed cases, with matched calls and caps, unequal input tokens, and zero updates. [Live traces and judge rubric](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/README.md#live-peer-turn-with-matched-calls-and-output-caps)

### Separate local relation, literal witness, and final heading

On four exposed claims, the first typed protocol rejected all 20 locally generated replies and openings. Peer and independent final prompts were then identical within each pair. Matching token inputs still produced one harmless paraphrase difference. Removing matching outer quote delimiters recovers ten exact excerpts, of which eight support the reported relation. Nine changed chair prompts were regenerated. Peer and independent raw briefs both average 51.5/70, and each gets 2/4 dispositions and 0/4 strict passes. [Protocol, exact review, and costs](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/README.md#separate-local-findings-before-writing-the-final-brief)

The peer reply recognizes that the US proposal for six months supports some extension of MINUSCA. The independent reply cites the same passage and calls the relation unknown. The peer chair still selects withhold. A post hoc heading rule yields 1/4 strict for peer versus 0/4 for independent and solo with the prose unchanged. The same rule makes two solo heading changes that rely on inadequate witnesses. Retain grounded local relation gains as a process measure, and report unsafe composition separately from a lucky heading match. Check both before using an outcome reward. All 41 generations replay with zero updates. [Heading-only replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-peer-quote-repair-evidence/header-replay.json)

### Learn a router from strict brief rewards and audit transfer loss

Two exact joint policy-gradient fits use the same twelve archived proposals, both assignments of the documentary slots, and the same outcome and payload costs. Their only treatment difference is acknowledgment access. After 180 updates per fit, training net is 0.171 adaptive versus 0.125 independent, from −0.053. The new adaptive policy beats its from-scratch independent control across six held-out cohort and holdings comparisons. It trails the older frozen router in all six. The largest split-holdings loss occurs on Gulf of Guinea and Libya, from 0.093 frozen to 0.063 fitted. Replacing just the responder gives 0.063, while replacing just the sender gives 0.092. This is transfer across fit-disjoint meeting families using prior generated brief scores, with zero language-model updates. [Fitting and transfer audit](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/README.md#fit-routing-on-archived-brief-scores-and-test-across-meeting-families)

Identical contradiction labels conceal different evidence semantics and rewards. Under split holdings on the six Gulf of Guinea and Libya cases, the exact adaptive ceiling is 0.096 from relation-only observations, 0.342 with the public claim type (vote or statement), and 0.366 with privileged case identity. The ceiling is optimized retrospectively. Use public claim type as a candidate policy observation and reserve new families for the transfer test. Judge linguistic evidence quality separately so that routing rewards do not conflate a recorded vote with a position on the scope of a treaty. [Exact ceiling and components](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-transfer-fit-evidence/attribution.json)

### Try public claim type with held-back cases and both document orders

The vote-versus-statement feature changes training return from 0.2132 to 0.2154 with the same 16 archived proposals and 180 updates. Its largest absolute transfer effect across six cohort and holdings comparisons is 0.00021. Gaza and Haiti/Mali were kept out of both fits. Gulf of Guinea and Libya were also fit-disjoint and had already motivated the feature. Neither type's fitting corpus has a case with two observed contradictions, the relation pair responsible for the previous diagnostic collision. [Matched fits and cell audit](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/README.md#train-with-public-claim-type-and-balance-the-document-order-at-evaluation)

The previously reported 0.342 type-aware ceiling on Gulf of Guinea and Libya used a single assignment of evidence to slots. Requiring one common policy for both slot assignments gives 0.121 type-aware versus 0.095 relation-only under split holdings. Most of the apparent headroom came from slot placement. Report order-balanced ceilings alongside the original order, and use matched receipt contrasts where two equal relation labels hide different evidentiary value. [Order-balanced exact audit](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-claim-type-fit-evidence/audit.json)

### Fill the two-contradiction training cell with receipt contrasts

Compose nine training-only pairs from previously audited single-card outcomes, including three true-versus-false statement pairs. Both learned arms use the same new receipts and both source orders. On the already exposed Gulf of Guinea and Libya diagnostic with slot-balanced split holdings, the fit recovers from 0.063 to 0.093, matching the frozen router with or without claim type. The type fit adds 3.24 percentage points of usable revisions and 1.08 points of false actions. Gaza and Haiti/Mali are essentially unchanged. The examples replay graded fields and deterministic composition, with no new prose judgments or model calls. [Construction and fitted scores](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/README.md#add-missing-receipt-contrasts-from-archived-single-card-outcomes)

The slot-balanced Gulf of Guinea and Libya ceiling rises from 0.095 to 0.224 when the desks receive a privileged correctness bit for each local relation. Adding claim type reaches 0.231. Aim for a usable evidence cue that distinguishes real objections from exact quotes with the wrong scope. Evaluate that cue on fresh meeting families before learning from it. [Trade-offs and oracle analysis](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-receipt-contrast-fit-evidence/audit.json)

### Hold the quote fixed while testing for a genuine objection

Use six paired witnesses from the Somalia and Western Sahara records, each with one compatible assertion and one incompatible assertion. Somalia joins the pinned UNSC-Bench release. Two frozen Qwen verification prompts score 7/12 each under strict format. Reasoning before the verdict passes two more grounded checks. Retrospective separator whitespace tolerance raises it to 10/12 correct. A bounded same-actor and same-resolution vote rule repairs the model's two roll-call errors and brings verdicts to 12/12, with 10/12 grounded and 4/6 fully grounded pairs. Keep the strict parse and outcome of each repair visible. [Cases, source joins and 24 replayed generations](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/README.md#verify-a-contradiction-against-a-fixed-witness)

On 24 paired-claim packets per family with both source orders, the literal selector produces 45.73% false actions. The hybrid verifier produces zero and yields 0.535 frozen net versus 0.537 acknowledgment-aware greedy under split holdings. The receipt benchmark evaluates justified objections using deterministic composition and same-assistant grades. Verify the semantic gate on fresh families, and retain the final-record and live peer tests as separate requirements for MARL. [Replay and exact comparisons](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-witness-contrast-evidence/routing.json)

### Carry a frozen witness verifier into a final Council brief

Test Gaza resolution 2728 and Myanmar resolution 2669 using official normalized evidence cards. Each joins UNBench author test with all 15 votes audited. Gaza also joins UNSC-Bench. The frozen hybrid verifier gets 7/8 local verdicts versus 6/8 for direct. All twelve final model outputs say retain even though the requested propositions require three revisions and one withhold. The model often correctly states the observed vote in the body and labels that corrected record as though it established the original proposition. Preserve the original claims for disposition scoring, and score collective adoption separately from each delegate's vote and policy position. [28 generations, full-record rubric and join audit](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/README.md#transfer-the-witness-verifier-to-a-full-council-record)

A post hoc roll-call composer earns two strict passes by binding each speaker's recorded act and the Council's adoption to the same resolution. Both statement cases still fail. The hybrid also misses France's explicit objection to concluding Council work and invents Norway's instrument preference in its explanation. Keep the deterministic vote composer as a positive factual control. Open-text verification needs to compare both clauses of the actual assertion before supplying a final-record reward. [Bound composition and failures](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-verified-brief-evidence/rollcall-composition.json)

### Score original attributions, then assemble the evidence record

On four exposed open-text claims, eight local source checks use the original attribution, its authored opposing alternative and an evidence-gap option. Across three candidate rotations per source, Qwen gets 18/24 choices right. Unanimity holds for just 2/8 sides. Falling back on unknown yields 3/4 dispositions and zero strict briefs in both generated-body arms. A post hoc majority returns 8/8 local relations. Five new source-local calls supply exact adequate sentences for every substantive finding. A composer preserves the scoped gaps and the Council decision and earns 4/4 strict. Count all 37 actual generations and preserve both failed writer arms. [Prompt pairs, evidence and full-record rubric](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/README.md#decide-the-original-attribution-before-composing-the-brief)

Replay the verified local fields with a public Council card and both document orders. Under split holdings the frozen router leads greedy 0.367 to 0.363 when reward counts strict retentions and revisions. Credit strict withholds too, and greedy leads 0.549 to 0.442. Greedy preserves more evidence in the correctly unresolved Myanmar brief. The absolute exact ceiling under this fuller reward is 0.550. This makes complete, source-faithful withholds a necessary reported outcome when comparing policies. The winner depends on the chosen reward and the test uses four exposed cases, authored alternatives, same-assistant review, and no new fit. [Replayed full records and rewards](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-attribution-extracts-evidence/routing.json)

### Train for evidence in justified withholds and measure revision retention

Fit two routing policies from the same initialization on 14 archived panels, with 180 exact policy-gradient updates each. Grade every receipt against the disposition warranted by the complete evidence and require every established side for strict credit. Two fitting panels reward supplying a known side to a correctly unresolved record. In the four composed test cases, rewarding strict withholds raises their split-holdings rate from 7.51% to 18.57% across all cases and raises full-record net from 0.115 to 0.220. Both fits deliver strict revisions in fewer than 0.1% of rollouts. The frozen router reaches 37.11% and greedy reaches 37.21%. Report retention, revision, and justified withhold separately so the aggregate can expose this tradeoff. [Matched fits and receipt evidence](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/README.md#train-for-justified-withholds-and-check-revision-retention)

A post hoc component swap uses frozen routing when a desk observes a contradiction and fitted routing otherwise. It restores the revision rate to 37.11%, keeps the withhold improvement and reaches 0.548 net. Acknowledgment-aware greedy reaches 0.549 and the exact adaptive optimum is 0.550. The guarded fit approaches greedy on Gulf of Guinea and Libya and trails frozen on Haiti and Mali. Keep greedy and frozen routing as controls. For the deliberation benchmark, require that a peer identifies which material source or objection is missing, that the reply supplies it with traceable evidence, and that the final record preserves it better than the matched solo, independent and direct-forwarding arms. The current routing exercise used previously exposed families, free acknowledgment and preassigned evidence. [Component swap and per-case replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-withhold-fit-evidence/guard.json)

### Test which source a question retrieves

Use the 24 May 2024 Council meeting on protection of humanitarian personnel to vary what the chair needs while keeping the other desk’s documents fixed. The first desk sees an editorial request. The responding desk sees two documentary cards and can deliver one. Where an independent desk sees identical documents for two different requests, a blind selector can deliver the relevant card on at most half the tasks. A query-aware selector can deliver the right card on every task. Score the resulting briefs separately. Credit the Council decision, the abstention, the task-specific attributed statement, limits of Council action, and source citations. Track whether the question caused the relevant card to reach the chair and whether the chair used it. [Protocol and frozen rubric](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-requested-evidence/README.md)

Compare the live generated question with a verbatim editorial query and direct forwarding of one or both cards. Include a three-pass solo analyst with both documents and record their different input costs. Keep the archive joins explicit. All 15 votes match UNBench author-test event 2531. The pinned UNSC-Bench release has no match by draft or meeting identifier. The official speaker statements and adopted resolution supply the final-record criteria. [Roll-call audit](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-requested-evidence/provenance.json) · [Official Council record](https://documents.un.org/api/symbol/access?l=en&s=S%2FPV.9634&t=pdf)

Frozen Qwen3.5-4B delivers the relevant evidence on 3/4 tasks with a generated question, 4/4 with a verbatim question, and 2/4 with independent selection. Peer finals meet the editorial requirement on 1/4, independent and verbatim on 2/4 each, and solo on 3/4. All 24 finals turn the Council's urging of States into a mandate, so every arm fails the integrity guard. The generated questions all reach their token cap. Require the final to preserve the operative verb and check the speaker, action, and recipient separately. Track routing success even when the final fails, and use verbatim-question retrieval as a control for gains from a trained question policy. [Recorded generations, arm-hidden review and scores](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-requested-evidence/README.md#frozen-qwen35-4b-result)

### Measure whether selective requests have room to improve

TarMAC learns what to communicate and which agents to address using downstream task reward. Wang and colleagues jointly learn which messages to transmit and how to schedule them under bandwidth limits. For a documentary test, preserve the verbatim editorial query whenever it fits. Give the clerk and the responding desks different knowledge about whether the requested evidence is already available. Use a common contact budget for learned routing, a script that forwards the same query and adapts to receipts, and independent desk summaries. Require each requested contribution to change a separately scored, attributable field of the final. Compute the best attainable outcome under the shared observations and budget before fitting. A case on which verbatim querying already reaches the best outcome can test fidelity and efficiency. [TarMAC](https://proceedings.mlr.press/v97/das19a.html) · [Learning Efficient Multi-agent Communication](https://proceedings.mlr.press/v119/wang20i.html)

### Check whether the final-record reward favors the relevant receipt

Replay each one-card view from the four Resolution 2730 tasks with tighter writing instructions and the same frozen checkpoint and final token cap. With the relevant source delivered, editorial coverage improves from 2/4 to 4/4 and mean record quality rises from 35.0 to 59.5 out of 70. All eight revised replies invoke “calls for” rather than preserving the operative “urges” on investigations, one also says “mandates,” and none meets the full citation contract. Two responses truncate. Keep legal modality, citations and completion as distinct checks. [Matched generations and exact review](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-authority-writer-evidence/README.md)

The original weighted reward prefers the wrong Russia card on the omissions task because that final renders the roll call more fully. The best blind selector ties the best task-aware selector at 38.5/70. The revised weighted reward favors the relevant card on all four tasks. Its blind ceiling is 52.5/70, and verbatim querying reaches the task-aware ceiling of 59.5/70. The strict reward remains zero across all measured views. Check the terminal reward across alternate receipts before fitting a messaging policy. [Exact replay and ceilings](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-authority-writer-evidence/results.json)

Resolution 2626 is a source-checked transfer candidate with a different operative action. The Council decided to extend the UNAMA mandate and separately requested periodic reporting. Russia explained its abstention by stressing host-country agreement and further cooperation with the de facto authorities. Authored tasks can require both the precise adopted authority and the attributed objection without treating the objection as a condition in the adopted text. The meeting joins UNBench author-test event 2430 and adopted UNSC-Bench row 238 with all 15 official votes agreeing in each. The following transfer test uses that source join. [Roll call](https://digitallibrary.un.org/record/3966592) · [Resolution and operative paragraphs](https://digitallibrary.un.org/record/3965463/files/S_RES_2626_%282022%29-EN.pdf) · [Verbatim meeting record](https://digitallibrary.un.org/record/3965484/files/S_PV.8997-EN.pdf)

### Test the learned source-request policy against guarded UNAMA records

Use an authenticated Council paragraph with the mandate decision and the separate reporting request. Ask frozen Qwen to write the supplement for each available subset of the Norway, Russia and China cards. Also give the free-form solo writer public evidence only and the full packet. The full-access free-form writer gets 40/70. The full-access writer with the fixed public paragraph gets 58/70 and fails grounding after reversing the role of opposition in Russia's explanation. A post hoc correction to leading citation labels and subjectless clauses raises guarded passes from two to five of eight receipt views, including the public-only view. The two desks can deliver at most two cards under the transfer budget. [Nine generation records, exact reviews and joined sources](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-unama-authority-evidence/README.md)

Replay the frozen joint policy trained on Western Sahara and DPRK under the original costs and 10% loss. Across all six slot assignments it scores 33.775 net guarded points after label normalization. The fixed script averages 33.242, exact independent achieves 46.457 and exact adaptive 47.299. In the original slot order its requests raise card delivery from 1.398 to 1.455 and reduce guarded net from 35.560 to 33.920. Score each attributable contribution before adding it to the final record, especially where one unsupported claim or an over-length supplement can invalidate otherwise useful evidence. These are receipt replays with typed communication and same-assistant review. [Per-mask scores, erased-message control and exact bounds](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-unama-authority-evidence/results.json)

### Fit against writer failures and check one pair to resolve tied sources

Run matched 280-update fits on archived Outer Space, UNIFIL, Mali, Myanmar and Climate receipt banks. Compare material credit, guarded credit, and guarded credit with the query disabled. UNAMA stays out of the fits. Use its singleton guarded scores to calibrate slot order and average over ties. On the normalized UNAMA records with uniform inventories, guard-aware learning raises net from 41.335 to 45.556 compared with material-score learning. The matched independent fit reaches 46.457. Norway and China tie when assessed alone. Their combinations with Russia differ sharply, and the two orders yield different communication outcomes. [Fitting banks, gradients and all source permutations](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-guard-calibrated-fit-evidence/README.md)

Reveal one of the two pair scores in a post hoc replay. In separate treatments, the Norway–Russia success and the China–Russia length failure each select the frozen table's stronger slot order. It reaches 47.208 net with uniform inventories and 45.891 with correlated duplicates. Exact planners with the same partial reward model reach at most 46.809 and 45.384 on the actual records. Erasing the query at equal cost drops 0.794 and 0.703 points. These effects use one previously measured pair in each treatment and exclude the other pair scores from selection. Evaluate the rule prospectively before using it as evidence of general transfer. [Pair-check procedure and scores](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-guard-calibrated-fit-evidence/pair-probe.json)

### Lock one-pair calibration before a new Council case

Resolution 2623 tests a current negative vote that did not prevent adoption, a veto at an earlier meeting, and a post-vote statement in national capacity by the person presiding over the Council. The new packet joins UNBench author-test event 2427 and adopted UNSC-Bench row 248 with all 15 official votes agreeing. Freeze the previous MARL and independent tables before generating records. Pick at most one pair to review for calibration, based on which pair's receipt probability varies most across the tied source orderings. Freeze the choice under uniform inventories and test the same order with correlated duplicate holdings. Keep exact planners with the same reviewed entries alongside full-table ceilings. [Locked protocol and release joins](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-emergency-session-evidence/README.md) · [Official meeting record](https://digitallibrary.un.org/record/3959142/files/S_PV.8980-EN.pdf)

All three singleton records earn 46 guarded points. Brazil–Russia and Russia–India fail the word limit, and Brazil–India falls to 42 because its supplement drops material. The locked one-pair check reveals Brazil–Russia and raises the frozen MARL policy from 33.045 to 35.535 net under uniform inventories. Erasing the paid query raises it to 38.061. The equally informed independent planner scores 38.855 and the full-table adaptive ceiling is 44.485. The UNAMA advantage fails to transfer under prospective selection. [All cases and matched controls](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-emergency-session-evidence/README.md#results)

Shortening only the common Council section from 87 to 58 words preserves all public rubric points and makes both long pairs usable at 50 points. The post hoc one-pair policy now scores 44.426, compared with 44.900 for singleton-only calibration and 45.631 for the equally informed adaptive planner. A measured pair of 50 is still below the additive prediction of 58, and the other pairs remain uncertain. Score departures from additive receipt value alongside factuality and the message budget before selecting a new training objective. [Compact public-section replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-emergency-session-evidence/compact.json)

### Compose independently reviewed notes to preserve earlier receipts

On the same UNAMA and emergency-session source cards, generate each delegation note separately and bound its length before combining notes. Bind its speaker and citation from that source. Keep the Council decision and vote in the fixed public section. Use the same reviewed note in every receipt mask that contains its card. This tests whether a second delivery adds the expected evidence without rewriting the first. Apply the word cap and source-local review before treating any result as a reward for communication. Freeze the transport policies and evaluate the same assembled record for each receipt mask. [Protocol and source joins](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-modular-record-evidence/README.md)

All six source-local generations are complete sentences, five exceed the locked 27-word limit and none passes the predicate gate. A post hoc sentence gate admits three unchanged, source-checked notes under a 36-word per-note allowance. All 16 final receipt views pass the factual and length guards. Full access supplies 58/70 on UNAMA and 46/70 on the emergency docket, with omissions in both. The measured scores equal the sum of the admitted singleton contributions throughout, so the one-pair check leaves policy selection unchanged. [Communicated notes, exact review and receipt tables](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-modular-record-evidence/README.md#results)

With uniform inventories the learned query scores 47.934 net on UNAMA, ahead of exact independent delivery at 47.470 and below the adaptive ceiling of 48.004. Request erasure at equal cost gives 47.426. On the emergency docket only one source survives review, and exact independence reaches the adaptive ceiling at 42.170. This shows how reliable admission and evidence composition change what the coordination task can measure. The measured gains use exposed cases, typed replay and same-assistant source review. [All scores and both inventory priors](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-modular-record-evidence/complete-sentences.json)

### Train a third delivery after the chair sees the first receipts

Give the chair one paid request for a missing card after two independent opening sends. The responding desk can send a third card whether it receives the request or hears silence. Run matched 240-update policy-gradient fits on archived Outer Space, Mali and Climate plus three authored all-source profiles. With UNIFIL and Myanmar held out of fitting, the learned query completes 13.21% of records under uniform inventories, compared with 9.11% after request erasure or with fitted independence, 11.16% for the fixed request script, and 12.53% for exact independent delivery. The two-send ablation completes zero. Exact independence still leads on net under both inventory priors. Report completion probability alongside the quality of partial records and the message costs. [Protocol, fit traces and matched controls](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-third-delivery-evidence/README.md)

On UNIFIL at uniform inventories, fixing the learned opening and optimizing the chair and reply against the evaluation table raises net from 52.850 to 54.166, above exact independence at 53.794. Using the exact independent opening and optimizing the same tail raises it to 54.898. The corresponding Myanmar values are 57.875, 58.893, 58.562 and 59.401. The improvement uses test-bank information and locates losses in both policy stages. Alternating the same exact optimizers on fitting data adds under 0.007 points on the two uniform transfer cases. Separate the room for coordination from how well the learned policy adapts to the document's actual evidence priorities. [Fixed-opening optima and fitting-only follow-up](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-third-delivery-evidence/README.md#diagnose-the-first-two-sends)

### Condition the third delivery on the public opening and rubric

Fix the first two sends using the exact independent plan for the declared rubric. Train the chair and responder jointly for 240 updates on other case banks and authored bonus contrasts. On UNIFIL and Myanmar at uniform inventories the learned tail reaches 54.881 and 59.202 net against 53.794 and 58.562 for matched independence. Erasing its query costs 1.119 and 1.254 points. The fixed request rule receives the same opening and scores 54.930 and 59.200. Public planning reaches the exact fixed-opening ceiling because the assessed receipt scores agree with the rubric on those two cases. [Fitting and matched controls](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-third-context-evidence/README.md)

A constant numeric rubric input leaves the learned scores unchanged in all eight case-prior comparisons. In a synthetic fixed-opening test, moving a 15-point bonus between source pairs changes the optimal tail and the learned tables remain identical. One shared policy incurs at least 0.340 net points of mean regret even at its exact optimum. On UNAMA and the emergency-session case, singleton review predicts the whole modular score table, but the newly calibrated learned tail still trails calibrated independence. Keep the public opening, the actual response to the query, and direct adaptation to numerical priorities distinct in the next comparison. [Pair contrast and singleton calibration](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-third-context-evidence/audit.json)

### Learn weights on the expected value of individual requests and replies

Keeping the opening identical across training tasks with different pair bonuses still produces identical tables on the held-out contrast. Replace that network with seven shared weights over action features. The chair estimates the value and answerability of each request using its receipts and the public opening model. The responder estimates the value of its legal sends using its own inventory, first attempted send and heard request. Under a 240-update joint fit the policy changes when the pair bonus moves. Mean regret across the two held-out contrasts is 0.079 points versus a minimum of 0.340 for any shared tail. Comparing with the initial weights isolates what training adds to the public estimates. [Paired and action-level fits](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-third-paired-evidence/README.md)

On the held-out UNIFIL and Myanmar records, the fitted weights beat the initial action rule, matched independent delivery and the fixed request script under both inventory priors. Uniform net is 54.988 and 59.293, within 0.064 points of exact public planning on each case. Erasing the request at equal cost lowers both uniform scores. The fit still loses to exact independence on the correlated UNAMA condition, where the writer admits fewer source claims than the rubric anticipates. All comparisons use typed transport and archived reviewed records. [Record coverage, raw scores and ablations](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-third-paired-evidence/action-values.json)

### Execute the learned third slot with an English request and payload

Use a common pair of opening sends and the same two frozen-model calls per policy arm. Translate the chair's selected topic into an English request. The responding desk decodes the topic, uses its local policy and transmits an authorized, previously reviewed note as text. Exact admission links the transmitted note to the final receipt record. On four selected branches, Qwen decodes eight of ten unique prompts and sends all six notes verbatim plus one silence. All twelve final receipt masks equal typed predictions. Two procedural-role decode errors are masked by the private inventories on those branches. Preserve decoding and end-record fidelity as separate metrics. [Execution protocol, raw trace and matched outcomes](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-live-delivery-evidence/README.md)

On the complementary UNIFIL branch, the learned and scripted requests both carry China's note and score 63, against 49 for independent delivery. All arms complete the full UNIFIL branch at 70. The selected Myanmar branches each expose a different preferred policy among learned and scripted requests. This is a controlled live language interface with approved notes and a locked assembler. The four branches deliberately illustrate contrasts and do not estimate aggregate policy performance. [Scored final records](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-live-delivery-evidence/results.json)

Name India's national and procedural capacities explicitly and rerun the two failed procedural query orders. Qwen returns digit 2 in both menus, correct in one and incorrect in the other. On a post hoc stress inventory with both relevant cards available, the correct decoding raises the learned route's score from 49 to 56 by adding the presidency note instead of duplicating the national position. Evaluate exact role recovery across menu orders and pair it with cases where a decoder error changes which evidence reaches the record. [Two actual retest generations and receipt replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-live-delivery-evidence/role-repair/results.json)

### Test new replies and a new final writer through the live route

Reuse the audited requests and the same four route branches. Ask the responding desk to rephrase only its authorized documentary card, and give the chair the resulting reply and earlier receipts. Compare with a writer that receives the original final source card, and a pooled solo writer. Nine of ten replies pass support review. China's live reply omits its support for UNIFIL, and the chair keeps the disagreement but loses the point of overlap. The original-card control preserves the overlap and incorrectly attributes China's sovereignty emphasis to Brazil. This separates facts lost in the reply from claims added by the final writer. [Paired custody and exact spans](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-live-writing-evidence/README.md)

Twelve of 16 distinct final generations hit the 220-token cap. The four that finish also fail factual review, so every live, source-direct and pooled arm has zero release points. The material-only sums on the four branches are 77/280 for learned and fixed request routing and 84/280 for independence. Source-direct totals are 98, 91 and 84. Preserve the cap and factual failures separately. The earlier typed-policy gains concern documented delivery. The new free-form endpoint introduces source and authority errors even when it receives the original cards. [Full record scores and trace](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-live-writing-evidence/results.json)

### Bind each final claim to a witness from the live reply

Keep the same routed source views. Render the public Council facts from the official-source cards and ask frozen Qwen for an exact witness for each specific national claim. Score the exact quote and its support separately. Compose agreement and disagreement clauses only when their required source claims are admitted. Keep a literal-admission arm to reveal cases where copying the right words still yields an unsupported inference. [Witness protocol and reviewed renderer](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-live-claims-evidence/README.md)

Nine of 14 exact-witness calls support their claims. Two correctly decline missing material, and three paraphrase the presidency claim and are rejected by the exact gate. All 16 assembled views are releasable and three UNIFIL views reach 70, including the learned live receipt. On the complementary UNIFIL branch, the learned route earns 56 from the live reply, versus 49 for independence and 63 when the chair sees the original card. The seven-point difference reflects China's mission support, which the reply omits. [Initial records and exact spans](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-live-claims-evidence/results.json)

Request sentence IDs separately for the presidency announcement and national explanation. The model selects all three available evidence pairs and withholds the national-only control. Replaying the resulting exact witnesses raises the learned live Myanmar presidency record from 49 to 56 and the full-access Myanmar record from 63 to 70. The unchanged route still loses the UK rationale on the other live branch. The diagnostic uses prewritten clause rendering and same-assistant review. [Reassembled records and verified sentence pairs](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-live-claims-evidence/role-ids/results.json)

### Recover omitted claims with matched generic and targeted requests

Keep the two preceding route decisions and source views. Rerun China’s UNIFIL reply and the UK’s Myanmar reply using a common instruction to preserve all material facts. Compare the original question, a question that names the missing category and the same targeted question with the supporting sentence removed. Both full-source arms recover the missing claims and score 63/70 on each route. The withheld-source controls stay at 56 and 49. The China targeted reply preserves “failed to fully address” more precisely than the generic wording, with equal rubric credit. [Six live rewrites and source-withheld controls](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-claim-requests-evidence/README.md)

Retain earlier approved claims even when they were dormant in the rendered record. Brazil’s checked mission support had contributed zero while China’s support was absent, and it supplies the conjunction after the new reply arrives. The original scoring code carried printed clauses forward and would have missed that seven-point recovery. The correction uses the already approved witness and preserves the original scoring result for comparison. This is a known-gap diagnostic with same-assistant review. It supports a completeness instruction for these two branches and leaves the incremental value of targeted requests unestablished. [Record and witness audit](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-claim-requests-evidence/results.json)

### Replay the full inventory and loss distribution on Resolution 2593

Freeze the action weights and the generic completeness instruction on a new Council docket within the Afghanistan agenda. Join the official decision and all 15 votes to UNBench author-test event 2397 and UNSC-Bench adopted row 243. Give desks simulated custody of sourced French, Chinese and Russian statements. The record gives 42 points to the Council action and its authority, seven to each delegation's account and seven to an evidence-backed comparison of the two abstentions. Evaluate all inventory and link outcomes under two priors. [Locked docket, provenance and protocol](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-prospective-2593-evidence/README.md)

Frozen Qwen3.5-4B decodes all three questions and silence. Only four of 12 generated replies preserve their authorized card under the locked gate. Five of six cross-delegation prompts introduce unsupported attribution or denial. With questions visible to the writer, the fitted route earns 53.269 and 51.690 expected final-record points, below independent delivery at 56.456 and 54.794. Replaying the same decoded requests and choices with the generated source-only replies recovers all typed receipts at unchanged cost. Fitted scores rise to 56.838 and 54.965. In that replay the fitted and initial rules tie on uniform material points, and the fitted rule gains 0.133 points under correlated duplication. [Exact replay and raw scores](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-prospective-2593-evidence/results.json) · [Question-blind reply ablation](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-prospective-2593-evidence/question-blind.json)

Score the query decoder, authorization, documentary coverage and speaker attribution separately. Include matched fallback questions that name a different delegation and a source-only writing control that preserves the chosen action. A router can improve access to evidence while the resulting language interface harms the record. The observed recovery uses a fixed renderer and same-assistant source review on one docket. The broader benchmark still needs family-level comparisons with resource-matched solo and independent-draft writers, along with calibrated judgments of which peer contribution changed the final prose.

### Give solo, independent and peer writers the same Resolution 2593 evidence

Use a common final prompt and give all three final writers the same sourced dossier. Match the budget at three working-note calls plus one final call. The two team arms share the first two notes. Give those notes to the third desk only in the peer lane. Let the pooled solo see the whole dossier at every step, and report the extra input tokens it consumes. Keep source-supported idea counts and the trajectory of each note separate from ten final-record criteria and the authority guard. [Protocol and exact-span rubric](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-matched-2593-evidence/README.md)

All three arms introduce the same 11 supported national ideas across their notes. The peer note added no supported idea relative to the independent draft. The independent and peer finals both cover 21/70 material points. The peer final adds a vague comparison and fails the authority guard by turning airport-security requests into a mandate. The pooled solo covers 56/70 and exceeds the 210-word limit by 30 words. Release scores are 21, 0 and 0 respectively. This exposed-docket comparison tests whether peer text helps given matched final evidence. The inputs are source paraphrases, the final judgments use same-assistant review, and no routing policy is trained or varied. [Exact scores and token budgets](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-matched-2593-evidence/results.json) · [Idea and uptake trace](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-matched-2593-evidence/process.json)

The blinded Qwen3.5-4B judge supplies 32 of 33 requested fields and agrees with the reference review on 21. It overcredits ten incomplete criteria and marks the invented airport-security mandate clean. Its longest judgment reaches the model cap before the authority field. Include separate authority and completeness calibration, an output-validity denominator and a hard distinction between a literal witness and evidence that covers every part of the criterion. [Runnable judge instructions and calibration audit](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-matched-2593-evidence/README.md#blinded-model-judge)

### Check whether a shared final revision can preserve coverage, authority and length

Run one revision each on the three Resolution 2593 records with the same source dossier and token ceiling. Compare a generic source-and-length rewrite with an explicit word allocation and a check of which actor performed each operative act. Keep an authored feasibility reference out of the model inputs. It earns 70/70 in 188 words under the 210-word cap. [Frozen protocol and reference](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-revise-2593-evidence/README.md)

Five revisions reach 63/70 and the sixth reaches 56/70. Every revision retains the criteria it started with. The structured instruction shortens each paired record by 22–35 words and removes the peer record's invented security mandate, which the generic revision repeats. All six revisions exceed the word limit and none meets the criterion comparing the abstention explanations. Even a post hoc count omitting headings and the docket citation leaves the shortest at 219 words. Keep the word constraint, exact operative authority and cross-source synthesis separate in reward calibration. This same-docket result uses six frozen generations and same-assistant review. [Paired criteria, release scores and raw generations](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-revise-2593-evidence/results.json)

### Fit reply-aware routing and check for collapse to silence

Give a failed reply the same message fee and zero new evidence. Keep opening decisions and authorized actions fixed. Fit the same seven action weights on clean delivery and on four reply-admission regimes. Replay held reward banks and the measured Resolution 2593 reply cache. Track query use, completion and fees separately, and compare against independent delivery and a planner that has the same public channel estimate. [Protocol and controlled fits](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-channel-fit-evidence/README.md)

The robust policy sends zero requests across all 28 cells. It avoids the brittle clean fit's failures via independent delivery and also gives up useful clean-channel requests. On Resolution 2593 with questions visible to the writer, independence is already within 0.034 mean net points of the exact planner with actual reply admissions. A post hoc conservative selector keeps requests in clean conditions and uses independent delivery in the tested noisy conditions. A wrong clean declaration lowers its question-visible score from 57.534 to 53.591. Treat the declaration as a testable assumption, and require improvement over independence under both accurate and mistaken reliability estimates before claiming robust learned communication. [Exact distribution check](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-channel-fit-evidence/audit.json) · [Policy-selection and misspecification replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-channel-fit-evidence/selection.json)

### Learn reliability from receipt feedback and price the probes

Keep the chair's observations to its own routing choice and the masks visible before and after the reply. Use four authored channel hypotheses to infer the reliability regime across episodes. Compare greedy posterior selection with an active probe every eighth episode, charge every probe and include independent, clean-trained and oracle-in-menu controls. Replay stable channels and clean-to-noisy-to-clean switches under both priors. [Locked feedback protocol and code](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-channel-online-evidence/README.md)

On measured Resolution 2593, independent delivery produces exactly the same receipt distribution in both channels. The clean fit produces distinguishable distributions, with 0.309 and 0.266 total variation under uniform and correlated inventories. The greedy correlated policy stays at its 25% clean prior and sends no requests. Under uniform switching it beats independence by 0.102 net points, then loses to independence by 0.988 under stable noisy conditions. Periodic probes add little under stable clean conditions and lose to independence under stable noise and both switch schedules. These are receipt-learning replays with known documentary banks, one random seed and no observed held-out conversations. A credible reliability result must show a useful signal before charging exploration to diplomacy episodes. [Mode separability, posterior and cost by phase](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-channel-online-evidence/results.json)

### Check reply reliability outside the evaluation docket

Use fictional cross-delegation questions as a six-call preflight. Freeze the selection rule and test the deployed reply interface on Haiti Resolution 2699, joining the official decision and all 15 votes to UNBench author-test event 2501 and UNSC-Bench adopted row 242. Score all possible inventory and link outcomes under both priors. Keep source attribution, predicate support and completeness separate from the mechanical preflight fields. [Protocol, source provenance and archived outputs](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-preflight-2699-evidence/README.md)

The frozen probe admits zero visible replies and two source-only replies. Normalizing one equivalent date spelling changes its selection from independent delivery to fitted routing with source-only writing. All four held query inputs decode correctly. Only the question-free China reply satisfies the full Haiti admission rule. Source-only writing improves the fitted route over visible writing on both priors and trails independence by 0.232 net points under uniform inventories while gaining 0.248 under correlated inventories. The Russia replies show why preflight must test the actual authority relationship alongside actor names. Each changes approval instead of after-the-fact presentation into approval before the Council receives the concept. The Haiti docket uses the same receipt weights as the prior Afghanistan docket, and all gains concern transport through a fixed renderer with same-assistant review. [Full scores and reply audit](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-preflight-2699-evidence/results.json) · [Date-format sensitivity](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-preflight-2699-evidence/date-sensitivity.json)

### Bind reply evidence to source IDs and test repeated false citations

Keep the Haiti router and its final renderer fixed. Ask Qwen3.5-4B to cite one exact source sentence ID for each required claim. Change the ID order, withhold the core sentence from matched decks, and test unsupported wording on the complete decks. One foil turns approval instead of after-the-fact presentation into approval before receipt. Read the source sentence attached to every returned ID and verify it against an author-approved mapping. [Protocol, decks and model outputs](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-cited-2699-evidence/README.md)

The model gets 15 of 18 fields on complete decks and falsely cites evidence for seven of nine absent targets. In two cases, both orders resolve to the same irrelevant sentence. Requiring all three correct fields in both reads admits only Russia. A post hoc merge of individually verified citations recovers Ecuador, China and Russia, rejects all seven false control citations and reproduces the typed transport ceiling. With that registry the fitted route gains 0.763 and 0.315 net points over independent delivery. The registry already identifies the authorized evidence, so compare against deterministic forwarding and report the cost of preparing verified mappings. Neither citation agreement nor valid IDs establish entailment on unseen sources. [Per-claim audit](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-cited-2699-evidence/results.json) · [Verified merge](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-cited-2699-evidence/union.json)

### Learn which private grade to disclose before certifying a package decision

Use a fictional UN workshop with a public domain, two private preference cards and three packages. Reject any package with a zero grade, protect the worse-off delegate, then use total grades to break ties. File one exact grade each and allow the chair one paid follow-up. Require a common permissible conclusion across all worlds consistent with admitted evidence. Fit both delegates on shared reward and compare with local lookahead, fixed package disclosure and all 6,561 pairs of deterministic local opening tables. [Protocol, fits and exact control](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/trained-warrant-evidence/README.md)

The fit ties the initial planner and the exact ceiling in all 36 named conditions. Both adaptive policies beat the best constant-package disclosures on reporting scope. On 32 unfiltered domains and two priors, the fit wins two cells, loses one and ties 61, with mean net 0.922284 versus 0.922069. Sixteen domains expose a sum-of-grades shortcut. A separate receipt ledger replays the named policy and verifies that each certificate is supported by its admitted grades. Use this as a controlled decision benchmark. A learned communication result needs measurable headroom over public lookahead as well as safe release. [All unfiltered conditions](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/trained-warrant-evidence/generalization.json) · [Certificate audit](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/trained-warrant-evidence/audit.json)

### Learn an unknown inventory correlation without loosening the evidence requirement

Hide the grade-card pairing from both opening policies and the chair. Give the learner earlier public grade receipts from a fixed balanced disclosure schedule. Compare a constrained matching posterior and an unconstrained EM estimate with uniform and true-prior planning. Score changes to the entire policy separately from changes to the chair's request alone. The release certificate continues to check every compatible card world. [Locked protocol and exact replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-prior-evidence/README.md)

On 32 synthetic domains, 81 calibration episodes raise mean net from 0.919702 to 0.925133 under the hidden single-match model. In the misspecified mixed-match condition, the gain is smaller and holding the openings fixed helps. Under independence, full replanning loses 0.001044 and changing requests alone ties the uniform baseline. The best single-match gain would need about 2,983 deployment workshops to cover calibration at an illustrative 0.10 points per report attempt. Separate uncertainty about the inventory mix from certification, and report the amount of observed feedback needed to earn the claimed gain. [All outcomes and observation costs](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-prior-evidence/audit.json)

### Carry a live grade request through to the certified final record

Select two capability branches from the frozen workshop planner. A follow-up establishes B in a maximin-versus-total tie case and impasse in the briefing case. Erasing that follow-up leaves either branch unresolved. Check the speaker, package and grade of each model-written report before delivery. Compare final prose from raw receipts, prose with the correct certificate supplied and a direct receipt-based renderer. [Protocol, cases and full records](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-live-evidence/README.md)

A complete-card prompt gets zero of four openings through the gate. Project the authorized grade before writing and replace the literal example with placeholders. All six reports then pass, and the chair recovers both intended decisions. The direct model has two of four decision labels correct and no releasable record. Supplying the certificate fixes all four labels and yields one releasable record. The Mali prose still confuses B's higher minimum with A being infeasible. Direct rendering from the admitted ledger preserves all four records with the correct authority, decision and explanation. These are exposed capability branches with same-assistant review. Keep sender authorization, decision certification and final explanation as separately measured stages. [Repaired language outputs](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-live-evidence/authorized-only/results.json) · [Deterministic comparison records](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-live-evidence/authorized-only/compiled.json)

### Test whether a feasibility table improves the certified workshop memo

Keep the decision certificate, admitted grade reports and output budget fixed. Add a table showing whether each package is feasible and optimal in all, some or none of the compatible worlds. Evaluate the prior live branches and new prose for paired receipt views from the three conditions where the learned and initial policies differ. Use the full condition distribution to interpret the selected branch. [Protocol, review and route audit](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-reasons-evidence/README.md)

The table repairs the original Mali explanation by preserving A's feasibility and B's better rank. Across eight distinct inputs per arm it leaves strict release tied at 3/8 while reducing correct decisions from 8/8 to 6/8. On one impasse example the writer calls C feasible despite the explicit table and overrides the certificate. Report repeated prompts, mechanical decision validity and prose justification separately. In the losing routing condition, the initial policy alone can certify 1.42% of outcomes versus 0.91% for the fit, although the first differing branch favors the fit. These are frozen-model outputs from selected fictional views with same-assistant review. [Deduplicated scores](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-reasons-evidence/summary.json) · [Full routing differences](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-reasons-evidence/route-audit.json)

### Add a third delegate and calibrate the strength of the policy baseline

Give three delegates private grades and allow one exact disclosure each plus a targeted follow-up. Continue to certify only decisions warranted by every compatible world. Fit all three opening policies jointly and compare with exact constant disclosures, public lookahead, coordinate optimization and a full-information ceiling. Use 12 held synthetic domains and two priors. [Three-delegate protocol and outcomes](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-three-evidence/README.md)

The fit ties public lookahead in 22 of 24 conditions, with one win and one loss. Coordinate search beats the fit in six and ties in 18. Removing the follow-up from the fitted policy costs about 0.148 net under both priors. The stochastic training objective rises from 0.807 to 0.890, while deterministic use of the unchanged initial action scores reaches 0.896 on training and 0.883 on held conditions. The initial softmax placed more probability on lower-value actions. Include a calibrated deterministic or low-entropy control before crediting such training gains to stronger collaboration. The independent replay verifies the fitted certificates across all held branches. [Training-scale control](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-three-evidence/temperature.json) · [Reconstructed certificates](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-three-evidence/audit.json)

### Adapt the chair from ordinary disclosures in unfamiliar teams

Hold the original certificate and follow-up budget fixed. Compare a chair that ignores the disclosure convention with a chair that learns it from earlier public receipt ledgers. Include familiar teams, a new mixture of familiar role policies, and a coordinate-search team outside the convention model. Give the learner no hidden cards or reward labels. Price the observation delay separately from message costs. [Protocol, exact branches and cross-play scores](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-requester-evidence/README.md)

After 16 histories the role-factored learner raises mixed-team net from 0.723555 without histories to 0.728372, close to the known-partner score of 0.728404. The grade-only baseline scores 0.719475. On out-of-model coordinate partners, a flexible per-role likelihood improves on the discrete convention model. A post hoc receiver that weights both models by reserved-receipt likelihood scores 0.728260 on mixed teams and 0.884670 on coordinate partners, above the grade-only chair in 18 and 12 of 24 conditions respectively with no losses in those two groups. The results use typed replay and stable synthetic partners, and the evidence requirement is unchanged by inferred disclosure strategies. [Role adaptation](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-requester-evidence/results.json) · [Unified model replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-requester-evidence/blend.json)

### Test requester adaptation with actual model-selected opening reports

Freeze the cross-play receiver on two new synthetic workshop domains. Give two model agents a private grade card and a disclosure convention, and give the third only the grade chosen by the fitted opener. The first interface admits zero of 16 choosing reports because the model writes package letters instead of numeric grades. All eight preselected transcriptions pass. In the repair, the model selects a package letter and the sender compiles its numeric grade. All 24 reports pass authorization, though the veto-first role follows its assigned convention in only three of eight choices. [Actual generations and verified reports](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-language-team-evidence/README.md)

With the repaired reports the blended chair improves three of four domain-prior conditions and scores 0.812804 mean net versus 0.814646 for grade-only planning. The true-partner planner reaches 0.817674. The earlier typed cross-play gains do not establish transfer when the language model deviates from the scripted roles. Report authorization, convention adherence and coordination performance separately. These exact replays use generated openings, synthetic calibration histories and mechanical follow-ups and certificates. [Per-domain policy outcomes](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-language-team-evidence/package-only/results.json)

### Require a baseline-relative gain across every compatible partner policy

Enumerate each delegate's deterministic disclosure tables. Keep those compatible with earlier public receipts, compute the least conditional expected gain of each request over grade-only planning, and change the default only when the lower bound is positive. Keep the publication certificate independent of this inferred policy. The bound requires a stable deterministic partner, valid grades before independent link loss and the declared inventory prior. [Protocol, bound and verified implementation](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-request-safety-evidence/README.md)

On the four exposed language-choice conditions, the receiver raises mean net from 0.814646 to 0.817642 after 16 ordinary histories. The known-partner comparator reaches 0.817674. Two conditions gain through improved certification and two through fewer unnecessary requests. The audit checks all 156 stable case-prefix combinations. Unannounced partner shifts still cause regressions in 30 of 768 histories after four observations and three after 16. These results use archived model choices and a typed follow-up. They establish conditional protection against model mismatch while a compatible true policy remains available. [Scores and shift controls](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-request-safety-evidence/results.json) · [Independent lower-bound check](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-request-safety-evidence/audit.json)

### Test conservative requester transfer on new model-selected teams

Freeze the baseline-relative request rule before generating 24 opening choices or reports on two new synthetic domains. All reports pass authorization. The veto-first role follows its convention twice in eight calls, so evaluate its actual choices and retain separate convention-adherence counts. Replay the full inventory and loss distribution under both priors from zero, four and 16 earlier opening histories. [Prospective protocol and complete outputs](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-safety-transfer-evidence/README.md)

At 16 histories, the conservative receiver improves three of four conditions and ties the fourth. Its mean net is 0.763436 against 0.761925 for grade-only, 0.760802 for the blend and 0.768982 when the actual partner tables are given. The first audited change skips a question. On the same opening receipts, the grade-only question elicits an authorized new model reply and reduces compatible worlds from 32 to eight. Both final certificates remain unresolved. The work establishes prospective transfer within two synthetic domains and verifies one selected live follow-up. Broader language deliberation remains a separate benchmark requirement. [Full replays](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-safety-transfer-evidence/results.json) · [Actual reply and final certificates](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/validity-audit/warrant-safety-transfer-evidence/reply-audit/results.json)

### Audit documentary rewards before adapting the request policy

Move the conservative request rule from fictional grades to six archived UN record banks. Topic-only openings leave every allowed policy compatible because singleton holdings force the same topic. A revised protocol preserves the independent third send in every arm and uses receipt frequencies to set a likelihood-based partner region. After 64 openings the region retains about 48–53 of 576 table pairs and the conservative mean gain over default remains below 0.00023 points per docket. On the emergency-session archive, posterior-mean planning under the incorrect declared rubric loses 0.761 points against the default. [Record banks, matched transport and uncertainty](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-conservative-archive-evidence/README.md)

Review the public record and each singleton receipt. Correcting those four scores reproduces full-bank planning on UNAMA and the emergency session, raising net by 1.135 and 0.494 points. A synthetic UNIFIL control moves the pair bonus while leaving the reviewed anchors unchanged. Singleton calibration then trails full-bank calibration by 1.362 points. Include targeted pair views when the rubric credits combining sources. The study reuses source-reviewed archives with simulated custody and independent loss. [Singleton and conjunction controls](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-conservative-archive-evidence/calibration.json) · [Exact branch audit](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-conservative-archive-evidence/audit.json)

### Choose pair and full-record reviews by their effect on requests

Start with four reviewed records and monotone receipt scores. For each candidate pair or full view, consider every possible new score and choose the review with the lowest remaining bound on nominal request regret. Compare with fixed and highest-visitation review orders under identical policies, and score the actual opening teams separately. [Protocol, score bounds and acquisition paths](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-review-selection-evidence/README.md)

Across six archived banks and twelve authored foils, two targeted reviews reduce the mean worst-case bound from 3.573 to 0.356 points, versus 0.531 for fixed order. Sixty-six of 72 conditions reach a half-point bound after two reviews, compared with 32 for fixed order and 40 for highest visitation.
