Actual-team mean net after two reviews is nearly tied between targeted and fixed order. The bound is conditional on the nominal partner model, fixed fallback and monotone reward. Check monotonicity when a free-form writer can introduce errors as it receives more evidence. [Independent grid audit and review counts](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-review-selection-evidence/audit.json)

### Measure final-record reward over every free-form evidence subset

Generate eight fresh Qwen3.5-4B final records for the exposed Haiti Resolution 2699 dossier. Across the 12 one-card additions, the strict material rubric records eight gains and four ties. All eight records finish within the length cap and fail release. Every record changes the reporting route from through the Secretary-General to reporting to that office. The Ecuador–China view also describes Ecuador as an abstainer. The source renderer scores 42–70 under the corresponding receipt rubric, while the free-form writer scores 21–35 on material coverage and zero on release. Use separate coverage and release metrics so that stronger coverage cannot hide a change of legal authority or a false attribution. [Protocol, exact spans and raw outputs](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-free-record-bank-evidence/README.md) · [Resolution 2699, paragraph 18](https://digitallibrary.un.org/record/4022890/files/S_RES_2699_%282023%29-EN.pdf)

With two further score reviews, the monotone planner bounds nominal regret on free-form material at 0.202–0.209 points across two opening priors. Independent score intervals give 3.350–4.076 points. These are conditional planning bounds and the observed release bank is flat. The common reporting error makes better evidence delivery unable to improve publication here. Test a writer that preserves the public mandate separately before reading a new communication result, and remeasure the release score for each receipt view. This exercise uses one exposed event and same-assistant annotations. [Acquisition paths and observed-team replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-free-record-bank-evidence/planning.json)

### Test whether preserving the mandate exposes a reward for suppressing evidence

Keep the verified 145-word Haiti Resolution 2699 record verbatim and use seven Qwen3.5-4B calls to produce only the national sections. Release moves from 0/8 to 3/8, including the public-only record and the two isolated abstainer accounts. Both two-abstainer comparisons attribute Russia's information concerns to China. Coverage improves along eight of twelve one-card edges, while release gains on two edges and falls on five. The public and Ecuador singleton scores already violate the monotone planner's assumption. Independent intervals bound nominal request regret after two more reviews at 1.910–2.082 points across both priors. [Composed records, exact spans and score replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-fixed-public-bank-evidence/README.md)

Even the requester with the complete observed reward bank asks zero questions under both nominal inventory priors, tying the baseline across team styles. Preserve authority and hold enough of the final writer constant to isolate gains in communication. Evaluate attributable positions and unsupported shared claims before giving any weight to a terminal reward that a policy can improve by avoiding evidence. This is a one-docket, same-assistant, typed replay. It identifies a failure mode of the reward and provides no fresh evidence of diplomatic generalization. [Request controls and release rates](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-fixed-public-bank-evidence/planning.json)

### Restore the request incentive with source-validated national accounts

Use six frozen Qwen3.5-4B witness extractions on the Haiti Resolution 2699 cards, including one withheld-sentence control per delegate. Each control reports the corresponding field absent. The exact checker rejects Ecuador's three original witnesses for replacing curly apostrophes. A post-run comparison limited to apostrophe equivalence admits all nine original fields. A source-reviewed renderer combines only approved accounts and compares the abstainers only when both qualify. Across eight receipt views, release rises from 3/8 with the free national rewrite to 8/8 with either gate. The full view reaches 63 with strict comparison and 70 with punctuation equivalence. [Raw witnesses, both gates and composed outputs](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-validated-accounts-evidence/README.md)

Under matched sender-attributed simulated transport, the score-known requester improves net over no requests by 0.323–2.753 points with the punctuation-equivalent gate. In a separate receipt-mask replay, the archived clean joint fit beats exact no-request by 0.315–0.763 points but asks after every opening, loses to initialization in one prior and trails joint exact planning in both. The robust fit ties no-request. Replacing its requester alone recovers 0.369 and 0.791 points with its responder held fixed. This is evidence of a usable reward surface and a specific learned-policy gap on an exposed case. The renderer and witness criteria are authored, transport is simulated and the fitting runs are archived. [Matched transport and trained policy comparisons](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-validated-accounts-evidence/transfer-apostrophe.json) · [Component interventions](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-validated-accounts-evidence/swaps-apostrophe.json)

### Train a chair against an identical no-request team

Hold both opening agents and the archived robust responder fixed. Train the chair for 96 CPU steps on terminal record reward and message costs, penalizing conditions in which requests lose to the identical no-request team. Across 30 distinct evaluation conditions, the trained chair gains 0.206 points over no requests. The untrained two-channel average gains 0.224 and the analytical degraded rule 0.210. The fitted chair asks 0.080 times per episode versus 0.195 and 0.116, respectively. It loses to the silent requester in eight conditions. The normalized Haiti and Afghanistan records produce identical simulated payoff surfaces and count once in that aggregate. [Fit trace, release controls and baselines](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-baseline-request-fit-evidence/README.md)

Across ten distinct score-bank and prior pairs, exact enumeration with hindsight finds gains of 0.191 for the best deterministic map with no channel-level losses and 0.235 for the largest mean without that requirement. Knowing which of the three simulated channels is active adds 0.045 points on average over the latter. More updates on these same receipt banks would leave the primary evaluation gap unresolved. Use distinct reward surfaces on unseen events, collect actual reply and evidence-admission outcomes and retain the analytical request baseline. [Exact maps and channel-value bounds](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-baseline-request-fit-evidence/channel-bound.json)

### Separate amendment disagreement from final consensus on AU funding

Use UNBench author-test event 2517 and verify both decisions from the Security Council record. The US amendment passed 9–0–6 with a French abstention. The amended Resolution 2719 passed 15–0–0. The pinned UNSC-Bench splits lack the event, correcting the overlap assumption in the prospective protocol. Distinguish the US case for a clear 75 percent ceiling from France's concern about predictable financing of the remainder. A French account contributes 7 authored points alone and 17 when both other sources are already held. The eight receipt ceilings differ from all previously used requester fitting and evaluation banks. [Case sources, provenance and judge instructions](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-resolution-2719-evidence/README.md)

With four calls per arm, interactive scores 58/70, independent drafts 57/70 and the pooled solo analyst 48/70. Only independent drafts pass release. The interactive final carries two traceable links from the discussion and repairs a wrong amendment tally in a note, then introduces an authority error and exceeds the length limit. The study matches calls and generation caps. Actual tokens differ, and the review is from the same assistant. On a separate ceiling-based simulation using the same event, the fitted requester beats silence in four of six conditions and trails the analytical degraded-delivery rule in all six. Keep these authored ceilings and live output scores distinct. [Live records and exact spans](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-resolution-2719-evidence/results.json) · [Planning replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-resolution-2719-evidence/ceiling.json)

### Validate what an exact quote supports, then ask for the largest missing criterion

Freeze the three Resolution 2719 note sets. Qwen3.5-4B proposes role-owned witnesses for eight claims. The host accepts 18 exact spans from the live notes. Twelve support their claims, four lack support and two are uncertain. The contradicted control even passes the host check with a quote naming France as author of the US amendment. The wrong-owner run falls back on the public anchor after a format error, and a separate well-formed proof swap verifies the ownership check. Case-local rendering preserves the public authority anchor, while semantic errors in admission still prevent release. [Role-owned witness audit and fixed erasure replays](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-2719-evidence-admission/README.md)

For one matched live follow-up, start with the six manually verified atoms. The largest single eligible improvement is France's willingness to discuss contribution levels from both the UN and EU. One targeted question obtains the exact evidence and raises coverage from 58 to 65. One generic question repeats a supported point and stays at 58. Withhold the decisive sentence and the targeted desk copies the earlier note instead of reporting absence. The source gate blocks that reply. This one exposed case establishes an observed value for a rubric-directed question with the scorer held fixed. The request policy is a heuristic and the baseline is same-assistant audited. [Three replies, costs and composed records](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-2719-followup-evidence/README.md)

### Transfer joint routing with a fully measured held-out reply matrix

Fit two joint routing policies at 96 CPU updates per arm. One uses recorded whole-card admission for Afghanistan and Haiti. The other assumes ideal admission with matching reward and transport. On Resolution 2719, generate all 12 question-by-source replies using the same interface and hold them out of training. Three pass exact-span review. The US source passes when asked the matched US question and fails under silence. The ideal fit gains 2.691 and 3.211 net points over the declared no-request team under the two priors. The measured-admission fit asks no questions and provides no gain. [Training and held reply matrix](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-measured-channel-evidence/README.md)

Replacing the ideal fit's responder with the other learned responder preserves its score. Erasing the question at the reply writer removes most of the gain, and erasing it from both the writer and the responder recovers paid silence. Flipping the one successful US-question cell leaves 0.067–0.086 points of advantage. Credit the measured value of that question–reply interface, with the joint learning and one-docket transfer claims left open. Whole-card gating also discards two incomplete US replies that would earn six conjunction points when Procedure and France are already present. [Ablations and replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-measured-channel-evidence/ablation.json) · [Partial documentary rewards](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-measured-channel-evidence/partial.json)

### Check question phrasing against stronger independent instructions

Generate two new targeted questions and two preloaded generic instructions for each Resolution 2719 source. The US card passes both new targeted questions and neither generic instruction, extending the original result to three matched questions and three failed generic or silent prompts. The French card passes one variant of each. With frozen policies and off-topic outcomes held fixed, the ideal-trained requester gains 2.40–2.93 net over hindsight no-request across matched phrasing scenarios and both priors. Updating only the generic prompt can reverse the uniform-inventory comparison, from +2.399 to −0.339. Match the strongest local authoring surface before attributing gains to inter-agent communication. The one-docket variants use actual model replies, same-assistant scoring and modeled routing. [Exact reply and policy replays](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-question-wording-evidence/README.md)

### Join Resolution 2504 across both benchmark releases and test release errors

Join UNBench author-test event 2310 and UNSC-Bench adopted row 245 to the official 11–0–4 roll call. The Russian oral amendment failed 3–7–5, the Belgium–Germany S/2020/24 passed, and Russia withdrew S/2020/25 after adoption. The five supplementary criteria distinguish the objects, their dispositions and the related source-owned arguments. The authored eight-view ceiling is 42, 46, 48, 52, 50, 59, 61, 70. [Verified join, sources and rubric](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-resolution-2504-evidence/README.md)

On one faithful record and five authored single-edit foils, a frozen Qwen3.5-4B release judge accepts the faithful record and cites exact changed spans in four rejections. It misses a sentence that promotes a requested assessment of humanitarian alternatives to a completed finding. Score procedure and evidence status separately, including requested, completed, adopted, rejected and withdrawn. This is a judge perturbation test with fixed source cards. [Raw judge decisions](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-resolution-2504-evidence/final-records.json)

### Transfer routing to Resolution 2504 with a strong independent writer

Use the joined UNBench and UNSC-Bench case and freeze both learnt policies. Collect all twelve question-by-source replies. The ideal-trained policy loses 3.351 points under uniform inventories and gains 3.599 under correlated inventories against hindsight no-request. Both values survive every card renumbering with semantic openings held fixed. In the main adverse branch, a question about Belgium loses the exceptional-mechanism qualification that the preloaded generic prompt preserves. In the main beneficial branch, a procedural question retrieves the missing third source and completes the record. [Live replies, comparisons and role order controls](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-2504-routing-evidence/README.md)

The strict reply rubric requires Russia's stated causal connection between adoption and withdrawal. Accepting correct chronology alone admits three more replies and preserves the trained policy's sign reversal. Under that sensitivity, the exact planner with an assumed ideal channel gains 0.140 and 0.297 on the two priors, while the ideal-trained policy yields −1.977 and +3.138. Fact-level rewards leave the sign reversal as well. Policy evaluation must specify the inventory law and the independently available reply prompt, and should retain direct planning as a comparator. This is a held reply matrix on a docket with prior benchmark authoring, with modeled transport and same-assistant review. [Chronology replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-2504-routing-evidence/chronology.json) · [Opening-state breakdown](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-2504-routing-evidence/origins.json)

### Preserve the first generic reply through a topical follow-up

Use two drafts per selected source for both learned routing and no-request. Keep the first admissible draft and forward at most one card. Collect the three missing second-generic replies on Resolution 2504 and reuse the documented Resolution 2719 pairs. All three new generic replies fail the whole-card gate, so the independent comparator benefits from preserving its first generic candidate as well. On Resolution 2504, retention changes the ideal-trained uniform gain from −3.351 to −0.309 under the strict rubric, and from −1.977 to +1.065 under withdrawal chronology. The correlated gains are +3.634 and +3.498. Resolution 2719 retains gains of +2.516 and +2.850 under either topical wording set. [Reviewed drafts, selector text and replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-retained-replies-evidence/README.md)

The two-call cap is shared per sent source. The learned policy uses 1.75 calls per episode while hindsight no-request uses 1.00 or 1.50. Reoptimize no-request after applying each per-call price. At one point per call, the protected Resolution 2504 chronology comparison still gains +0.411 under uniform inventories and +2.297 under correlated inventories. These are modeled net values with verified draft labels supplied to the selector. Release depends on the accuracy of that admission step, which remains unresolved for a live automated judge. [Call counts, tokens and price sensitivity](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-retained-replies-evidence/costs.json)

### Measure the cost of replacing reviewed admission labels with a judge

Evaluate the retained-draft selector on all 14 distinct Resolution 2504 drafts. The first exact-span judge admits nine under chronology scoring, four of which fail the prior review. In correlated inventories, an overcredited generic procedural draft displaces a valid topical reply. Trusting the judge's approvals inflates the ideal-trained net by 4.456 points, while audited gain over no-request falls to −0.499. Report actual selected text and separate audited scores from apparent automatic reward. [First gate, exact spans and replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-retention-judge-evidence/README.md)

A targeted second pass removes all four false approvals but also rejects two of five reviewed passes. The correlated net recovers from 51.489 to 55.447, near the reviewed-label result of 55.486. Uniform net falls from 58.857 to 55.501. With the two-pass gate, the trained comparison is −0.223 and +3.487 relative to hindsight no-request. Review cost rises from 1.75 to about 3.2 judge calls per episode. This is a repair after inspecting errors on one exposed docket, and the same-assistant labels remain the comparison reference. [Second gate and resource tradeoff](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-retention-challenge-evidence/README.md)

### Test whether a case-neutral second judge transfers

Apply the exact-span gate to all 18 distinct Resolution 2719 drafts from the retained-reply interface. The first judge admits five of eight reviewed passes and three of ten reviewed failures. A case-neutral challenger modeled on the Resolution 2504 repair returns CLEAR for all eight initial positives. Both full and selective challenge policies leave every selection unchanged. Full challenge adds 0.76–0.81 calls per episode and selective challenge adds 0.26–0.52 above the initial 1.75. [Raw judgments and selection replays](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-judge-transfer-evidence/README.md)

The original questions score −0.206 and −0.184 over hindsight no-request under automatic admission and audited rewards. The alternate questions score +2.629 and +0.703. Crediting the judge's decisions without audit gives 3.24–6.08 more net points than the reference supports. A perfect recheck of first-stage positives restores some credit but leaves a 1.271–3.053 point shortfall from the first stage's false rejections under the original questions. Benchmark selection and review must measure both precision and recall, as an accurate filter applied only to positive verdicts has a fixed recall ceiling. These figures use same-assistant labels, simulated transport, and an exposed case with held gate responses. [Positive-only bounds](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-judge-transfer-evidence/positive-bound.json)

### Publish only source passages registered to documentary fields

With two exposed UN cases, collect 24 source and question combinations and six withholding controls. A host checks exact passage IDs against a registered source map and emits only supported fragments. The ordered contract gives 15 complete cards. Resolving the same replies by passage identity gives 19. The host emits no absent field across six withheld tests, even though the model explicitly abstains on the correct field just once. Resolution 2719 has full evidence delivery under identity decoding. The frozen fit's +0.696 and +0.242 gains over hindsight no-request then follow source routing without any variation in reply admission by question. [Passage-level evidence and whole-card replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-passage-routing-evidence/README.md)

For Resolution 2504, preserve the tally and its explanation in one source sentence and accept a set of passage IDs. The combined repair recovers all four procedural views. The withheld view emits sequence alone. In the resulting mixed replay, the fitted chair gains +0.832 and +0.143 over no-request. The declared-ideal planner gains +0.937 and +0.526 with fewer requests. These results test deterministic evidence admission with a pre-authored source catalog and modeled delivery. They keep open-ended judgment, learning gains and transfer to unseen document families open. [Bundled procedural certificate and comparisons](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-procedure-passage-evidence/README.md)

### Learn first disclosures and follow-up on the same reward

Fit both opening senders, the requester and the responder with exact expected team reward. Compare with a fit that holds the opening fixed and a direct planner that alternates between optimizing the opening maps and the follow-up. Use 30 archived development scenarios for fitting and replay source certificates from Resolutions 2719 and 2504 under two inventory laws. Each fit takes 96 CPU updates. The joint fit retains every fixed-opening action. Removing the initial reference bonus recovers those same openings and changes only follow-up decisions, adding at most 0.014 net points over the fixed-opening fit. [Protocols and policy replays](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-passage-joint-fit-evidence/README.md)

The joint fits gain 0.143–0.832 net points over hindsight no-request. Direct planning using the declared delivery model gains 0.560–0.950 and asks in 66–68% of episodes, while both greedy fits ask in every reachable episode. A feature-capacity audit reproduces the declared planner's opening maps across the four conditions under the current tie rule. One sender's pooled fit relies on ties. A bounded imitation control could test whether planner opening actions survive the fit and whether changing those actions improves decisions with the same follow-up. These are structured CPU policy updates and simulated delivery on two exposed cases. The live final-record comparison against matched solo and independent drafts remains a separate evaluation. [Fit traces, opening constraints and costs](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-passage-joint-fit-evidence/README.md)

Swapping in only the planner's opening maps improves three of four rows. Keeping fitted openings and swapping in planner follow-up sends one correlated row to 4.278 points below no-request. The complete planner scores 0.560 above no-request in that row. A training target for coordinated disclosures should account for how they change what the chair expects to receive. [Planner and fit component swaps](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-passage-joint-fit-evidence/planner-swap.json)

### Recompute what a follow-up can recover when opening agents change

Refresh the chair and responder's public model of the opening messages at every fitting step. In a 96-update CPU fit, soft development reward climbs from 47.878 to 53.098. The greedy policy picks the fixed-opening actions on all 64 sender and inventory decisions and asks a follow-up every episode. It ties the fixed-opening fit in all four held replays and trails the declared planner. [Protocol and policy traces](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-passage-adaptive-fit-evidence/README.md)

Swap in the planner's opening maps and refresh each chair's public opening model. This helps both fitted policies on the two correlated conditions. The earlier static fit gains 0.134 and 0.058 net points from the refresh, versus 0.117 and 0.026 for the new fit. Every fitted chair still asks every episode. The evidence supports refreshing context when opening behavior changes. It leaves learning when to ask as the gap to the planner. An improvement test should target the value of a question against silence at each reachable receipt, with the opening and continuation fixed for that counterfactual. [Opening intervention](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-passage-adaptive-fit-evidence/opening-intervention.json)

### Reverse who speaks first before crediting peer deliberation

A matched free-writing test on the Gulf of Guinea and Libya used a source desk pair and a facilitator. The second desk either worked independently or saw the first note. Pooled solo had both sources on both passes. In both speaker orders, peer visibility improved the Gulf of Guinea brief over independent drafts (+20 and +30 points out of 70). With China first, the peer-informed Ghana note restored Norway as co-penholder and the final retained it. On Libya the peer advantage was zero in both orders. With the UK first, the peer-informed Algeria note recovered the full withdrawal demand but the final dropped it. Reversing the display of identical independent notes changed the final score by ten points on each docket. Keep decision usefulness, source ownership, new information, and final retention separate. One equal-score Libya comparison reverses sign under plausible criterion weights. These are 12 actual briefs, two exposed dockets, one frozen model and same-assistant review. [Order-paired records, review and cost audit](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/chair-brief-crossover/README.md)

Holding the learned opening maps and responder fixed, a chair that computes the net value of each question against silence improves all four held conditions. It gains 0.017–0.334 over the all-ask fitted chair using the declared channel, and asks in 47–74% of episodes. The measured-channel optimum supplies little extra value on these rows. The gains come from changing the question policy while holding the learned behavior of the other agents fixed. This gives a statewise supervision target and an analytical comparator for a learned chair. [Per-receipt counterfactual values and exact replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-passage-adaptive-fit-evidence/silence-counterfactual.json)

### Fit question value and check whether the chair learns to stay silent

Use the exact question-versus-silence advantage as a signed target for a three-feature chair with fixed teammates. The fitted rankings improve three of four held replays with fitted openings, by 0.004, 0.152 and 0.320 net points. The fourth loses 0.032. The chair still asks every episode. A separate cutoff fitted on development return changes no held decision. Its six grouped development checks produce four ties and two gains below 0.003. The exact declared-channel comparator with the same teammates asks in 47–74% of episodes. The original features estimate value through a scripted responder, while the target is defined by the frozen learned responder. A feature or critic using the learned responder's expected evidence deliveries is a more direct test of the remaining mismatch. [Signed fitting targets, held replays and cutoff checks](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-passage-advantage-fit-evidence/README.md)

### Evaluate question value with the learned responder and check ambiguity in the public rubric

Compute expected receipt changes from the frozen learned responder. Fit separate material and completion weights while preserving known delivery costs. The responder-aware chair asks in 47–74% of the passage episodes and improves every condition over the previous fitted chair. The fitted weights reproduce the uncalibrated analytical action maps throughout. Across four additional archived scored banks, both variants beat the earlier chair in six of eight conditions and lose to silence in the two emergency-session conditions. The gains come from modeling what the responder sends. [Policy comparison and receipt-level traces](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-passage-responder-critic-evidence/README.md)

The emergency-session and UNAMA banks give the chair identical public scores but award credit to different source cards. Exhaustive comparison of 864 legal request maps per inventory law finds a shared policy with at least +0.063 and +0.020 net gains over fixed-team silence across the two cases, using both scored banks in hindsight. Even the lowest worst-case regret among shared deterministic maps is 0.118 and 0.093 points. Treat those as limits of the supplied observation and fixed teammates. A grounded request test should record the public descriptions of what each source can establish and verify whether a small number of reviewed receipts resolves the ambiguity. [Reward-ambiguity audit](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/facilitator-passage-responder-critic-evidence/reward-alias.json)

### Test targeted retrieval and final-record retention on Bosnia Resolution 2604

Match UNBench author-test event 2408 to UNSC-Bench adopted row 251 and the official meeting. The Council unanimously authorized a twelve-month EUFOR ALTHEA renewal. The British and Russian statements disagree on the civilian High Representative. All four drafting paths use four calls and the same frozen Qwen3.5-4B writer. The adaptive path asks for a condition missing from the independent notes. It recovers and retains the UK's conditional support for executive powers. The final drops the 5+2 closure condition that it already had and changes keeping the matter on the agenda into a claim about asset seizures. It scores 62/70 and fails release. The fixed UK and Russian supplement paths score 36/70 and 63/70 and pass release. Pooled solo scores 28/70 with an uncertain source guard. Record each criterion's appearance in the opening notes, follow-up and final. Use the strongest fixed supplement as a comparator rather than treating a successful targeted answer as proof of better final writing. On the four masked outputs, the full LLM judge exhausts its 500-token cap in every call. The compact prompt yields three valid judgments and recognizes a Russian-dialogue criterion missed in the first reading of the adaptive final. It also misses the asset-seizure claim. It gives full points to the fixed Russian follow-up using an exact span about the UK's support for military renewal as evidence of civilian executive powers. Validate schema, quote fidelity and semantic support separately. [Judge prompts and verdict audit](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/bosnia2604-routing/README.md)

### Clarify one public phrase and replay the same Bosnia notes

Replace “remaining seized” with “keeping the matter on the Council's agenda” in the final writer's public anchor. Four new frozen-model finals reuse all opening and supplemental notes. The adaptive record removes the asset-seizure claim and passes release, while coverage falls from 62 to 48 as it drops the conditional use of executive powers and the internal scope of Bosnian dialogue. The fixed Russian follow-up still leads at 56 and passes release. The fixed UK final retains 36 but fails release after attributing the vacancy to interference in dialogue as well as appointment procedure. Pooled solo gains seven coverage points and remains uncertain on release. Treat public phrasing as an evaluation condition. Compare recovery in the follow-up, retention in the final and source-supported release before attributing a terminal reward change to the dialogue policy. [Paired outputs, verified prompt change and source spans](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/bosnia2604-routing/plain-anchor/README.md)

### Hold the Council decision fixed and isolate the value of one qualification

Supply the same verified 53-word decision across all Bosnia Resolution 2604 paths. Ask the frozen writer to compose only the delegates' section from the archived notes. Score the resulting sections out of 30, with 40 separately supplied public points. The adaptive section retains 30/30 and passes release, and the strongest fixed path retains 23/23 and passes. The former leading fixed Russia path retains 15/23 and fails after changing the actor responsible for the 5+2 closure request. Pooled solo retains 0/7 and remains uncertain on release. The adaptive body uses identifiable speakers and omits explicit delegation names. [Available evidence, composed records and release review](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/bosnia2604-routing/protected-record/README.md)

In two further calls, removing “if needed” from the adaptive note costs exactly seven delegation points, and inserting it into the fixed UK note adds exactly seven. The other criteria and release status are unchanged. This directly tests whether the final improvement follows a piece of retrieved evidence under a fixed writer contract. Use missing-field interventions and source-attribution guards to verify credit assignment before fitting a routing policy to terminal scores. This is an exposed one-case result using the same checkpoint for all generations and same-assistant review. [Two exact note edits and scored consequences](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-kit/reader-use/bosnia2604-routing/protected-record/qualification-swap/results.json)

### Follow frozen learned routing through fresh Resolution 2719 records

Use two predeclared reachable worlds on the archived UNBench author-test case. In one world the learned question switches the responder from a duplicate US note to France. The final retains France's objection but drops its willingness to discuss UN and EU contribution levels. It scores 6/28 and passes release. A fixed France supplement retains both fields and scores 13/28 with a pass. The archive excludes this case from the fit but includes prior benchmark development on it, and the worlds condition on successful transport. Treat the result as a downstream routing diagnostic. ### Measure how much of Resolution 2719 survives a verbatim word budget

Use the six fixed writer inputs from the learned-routing replay. Deduplicate repeated notes from the same desk and enumerate exact sentence prefixes, including desk headings in the 90-word limit. Reviewed extraction reaches 17/28 for the learned and fixed-France source-switch paths, 11/28 for silence and 17/28 for both wording-only paths. The pooled result repeats the vote across sections and reaches 128 words. The 90-word reviewed extractive controls therefore outperform these generated records where the inputs contain complete bundles. Measure evidence availability and priority selection independently of the protected writer's final score. Across 30 development banks, fit two decentralized teams for 96 CPU updates each with and without 70-, 90- and 110-word limits. ### Run the frozen v1 benchmark on the Syria workshop pair

Frozen Qwen3.5-4B gets zero of twelve exact decisions across the peer, independent and pooled arms. On the overlap case, independent notes preserve both requirements in both orders. With Brazil first, the peer-informed UK repeats a recommendation for A and simultaneously states its incompatible checkpoint requirement. With the UK first, the peer-informed Brazil note drops its nine-month requirement. All six impasse cases disclose enough to exclude every package. All six final writers still label the result unresolved. Train and score truthful retention by the responder and certified disposition by the chair as separate targets. [Verified baseline, exact spans and records](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/syria-baseline/README.md)

### Carry the peer constraint without the attached recommendation

Across four Syria peer episodes, a quote-anchored projection restores second-desk mandate retention from 3/4 to 4/4, removes one false local endorsement and raises exact final decisions from 0/4 to 1/4. Both overlap finals still miss Package B. In the successful UK-first impasse, crossing the old and new writer inputs shows that each individual edit still produces `UNRESOLVED`. With the fresh Brazil reply held fixed, restoring the old opening changes only the final decision line. Separate credit for constraint transport, retention and the final classification. ### Fit a joint language-conditioned decision policy and keep disclosure in training

Train two action heads jointly over frozen Qwen3.5-4B features on 16 synthetic workshops. The original fit raises return and still makes only 2/8 supported correct decisions on fit-excluded Iraq and Syria conditions. Mixing full truthful disclosure into half the training mass yields 6/8, versus 8/8 for fixed disclosure plus exact readout. The fitted policy chooses full disclosure throughout, and both Syria overlap orders remain wrong. On archived Syria note pairs the mixture chair recognizes all four certified impasses and makes unsupported commitments on all four uncertified overlap notes. Joint reward can improve this bounded controller, and calibration under partial evidence remains a failure. ### Train deference to missing disclosures and test a visible contradiction

Mix one quarter truthful disclosure, one quarter withholding and half learned policy mass. The fitted team keeps 6/8 supported correct held decisions. Fixing withholding now yields 8/8 appropriate deferrals, up from 0/8 with a complete-disclosure-only mixture, while uncertified archived overlap deferrals improve from 0/4 to 1/4. Add paired chair examples in which one package letter makes a public endorsement contradict the same speaker's mandate. The new fit copies frozen Qwen's choices on all sixteen held paired records, lowers supported correct deployments to 2/8 and loses all four archived impasse decisions. Report how many paired contrasts change appropriately and keep supported action rates beside deference rates. ### Read public constraints before committing the chair

Use frozen Qwen3.5-4B to extract attributed constraints and endorsements from 14 archived Syria notes and eight matched stress notes. Literal quote validation gives 3/4 appropriate deferrals and 2/4 correct certified impasses, and accepts a proposal as a binding constraint and a peer's mandate as the speaker's. Requiring binding wording and ownership clears 8/8 stress notes but suppresses all four archived impasses. Recovering the surrounding clause admits one projected impasse. Scanning explicitly owned clauses recovers both projected impasses and all four appropriate overlap deferrals. The balanced learned chair resolves all four impasses and defers appropriately on one overlap. Reliable public evidence extraction and a chair's willingness to commit remain separate evaluation targets. All sixteen frozen Qwen3.5-4B clarification replies correctly report their speaker's private requirement. One question recovers both missing Syria impasses and cannot certify any overlap given the starting proof state. The synthetic-trained router asks in all eight records and ties always-first routing, including an erroneous A recommendation that combines truthful new Brazil disclosure with a stale UK endorsement. Require explicit current compatibility for affirmative proof and query only when a valid answer could complete a certificate. ### Carry public certificates into the Iraq final record

In both Iraq workshops and both speaker orders, two frozen model declarations produce eight truthful local mandates and four correct public certificates. The independent and typed-notes-only writers both score 0/4 exact first lines. Adding the computed certificate improves this to 2/4, with the other two agreement outputs spelling the right option as `PACKAGE-A`. Clarifying the literal label format alone yields 4/4 exact and four released records, with one full-rubric pass. Both revised impasse records still omit unanimity and the documentation choice. Measure correctness of the public proof, terminal format, and full source-faithful coverage separately. ### Compare learning and exact planning when two recipients can complete a proof

Fit a joint chair and responder with both compatibility and delivery probabilities varying. The learned team earns 0.6781 on the held rate grid, above the fixed proof-relevant rule's 0.6405 and below the probability-product control's 0.6817. With the same learned chair, an exact responder yields 0.6816. The fitted responder sends all 1,024 reachable negatives that cannot close the proof. A forced-recipient training mixture leaves those actions unchanged. Directly fitting the responder on the sign of its counterfactual send-versus-pass reward removes all these errors and reaches 0.6816 without changing any selected recipient or certified outcome. The controlled grid uses Iraq and Syria package IDs and models delivery. The duplicated menu structure measures transfer over rate grids, with no new language calls. [Matched controls, learned policies and replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/competing-clarifications/README.md)

### Let the language responder choose its own signed claim

On 44 held Iraq and Syria prompts, a reward-trained Qwen3.5-4B action head raises potentially certifying truthful replies from five to twelve and introduces four false signs that could certify a wrong decision. Splitting compatibility estimation from send-or-pass makes only a small difference and retains four false signs. Verify the sign against the replying role's own typed constraint before admission. Rejecting the four contradictions preserves all twelve truthful replies and raises held net utility from 0.289 to 0.397. The verifier needs typed local mandates and public package fields. Group both alternative recipient prompts under a single public observation and a one-question budget. With typed sign verification, the joint fit yields 38.72 expected new proofs on 120 held rate presentations, using 104 questions and sending 48 unproductive replies. The calibrated public-value planner obtains the same proofs in 80 questions with an exact endpoint. Fitting each question's advantage over silence recovers those 24 question savings with the original responder. Directly fitting the responder saves another 16 unnecessary sends but drops four potentially certifying agreement replies. Expected impasses stay at 20.88. ### Test a native Qwen reply adapter against the verified controller

Fit two rank-four adapters for 12 updates each on synthetic prompts, with all Iraq and Syria presentations excluded. The native expected-reward fit reduces useful held replies from six to one. A dependent class-balanced counterfactual-credit fit preserves six and raises forced-recipient utility from 0.3049 to 0.3105. In the fixed public-planner replay, its net drops from 0.3201 to 0.3165 and expected proofs drop from 14.90 to 14.14. The previous feature head with a typed local guard yields 29.80 expected proofs and 0.4216 net. Neither native fit improves that controller. Keep the response policy, public route and certificate metrics distinct when reading any training gain. The v1.1 gate requires a correct workshop decision for a record to release. In the 16 archived Iraq finals, independent writing and typed notes alone score zero released points. The computed certificate yields 56/280, and the same certificates with the literal decision-line instruction yield 210/280, all four exact decisions and one full-rubric pass. ### Check whether a learned selector forwards better bundles

Ten archived writer banks across seven families give the exact score for every subset of three available notes. A ridge fit using only the held bank's empty and singleton reviews raises released coverage over uniformly weighted receipt masks from 41.46 to 42.54, while lowering downstream modeled net utility from 45.88 to 43.51 under uniform inventories and from 44.74 to 42.80 under correlated inventories. Planning with all reviewed bundle scores reaches 50.42 and 48.37. The result shows why a selector should be scored after composing it with the disclosure and request policies. Revealing one archived bundle score improves the ridge-directed plan from 43.94 to 47.11 net under uniform inventories and from 43.26 to 45.74 under correlated inventories. Omitting Haiti 2699 as well reverses both gains. The seven-family replay gives no reason to prefer learned review selection to the fixed-pair control. ### Estimate delivery while allowing for deliberate silence

A public rate estimator marginalizes over private requirements and the learned sending gate. It checks the likelihood of 283 terminal trace patterns. Across 18 degraded conditions, the mean 24-workshop net improves from −1.011 with nominal selection to −0.078 with the correct update. Naive updating stops sooner and does better under degradation, but costs reward in the stable controls. An 80% posterior profitability gate has seven negative degraded conditions versus 13 with unconstrained reply-aware selection. It gives up two profitable stable cells. Selective compulsory probes recover one of those cells while raising losses under degradation. The comparison assumes fixed candidate teams, known optional-reply behavior and rates that remain stable across workshops. [Full paired runs and trace audit](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/source-aware/infer-delivery/README.md)

### Run the learned team through one interface and test a different menu size

The runner reproduces the two scored impasse records from exact archived Qwen replies. Dropping or invalidating either decisive reply produces a deferral in all eight fault tests. Desk callbacks receive one fictional private card, and the chair receives only accepted public signs. The compiler matches the existing record path for all 81 states across the three packets. [Interface and replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/runtime/README.md)

Both policies were fitted solely on four-option menus. Across 56 three-option menus, three delivery profiles and two caps, public selection between the two learned pairs and silence beats fixed order on mean net in every rate-and-cap condition. It loses to fixed order on eight individual menus with even delivery and a three-question cap. Exact planning remains ahead and the maximum net shortfall is 0.0697. This is a structured size transfer with known delivery and the same private sign protocol. [Per-menu results](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/runtime/size-transfer.json)

### Replay the historical router with a verified, concise final record

On Resolution 2422, all three native desks copy their exact two source-packet sentences inside an echoed wrapper. The original flat parser admits zero. A narrowly addressed normalization recovers all six without extra calls. Exact sentence assembly reaches 58/70 under the frozen cap, while a compact authored form reaches 70/70. The frozen learned route again beats silence with the same responder, unlike the earlier free writer. The best fixed request, for Serbia's evidence, beats the learned route in all three inventory laws. With the full rubric and fixed openers, the recomputed exact no-request policy also beats the original learned team. [Native receipts, compiler results and exact comparator](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/README.md)

### Align historical routing and its controls to the same final score

The earlier Resolution 2422 exact helper added a 21-point completion bonus while learned and fixed arms used released points alone. Recomputing the exact controls on one objective preserves the old learned route's shortfall. A joint fit on synthetic rubric banks, followed by public-model pruning of unhelpful requests, beats the best fixed ask and exact no-request in all six historical rows. It cuts the query rate from 100% to 13.6–38.8% and remains 0.045–0.055 net below exact adaptive planning. On 108 held synthetic conditions the pruned fit beats the best fixed ask in 69. [Objective audit, held comparisons and replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/README.md)

### Transfer historical routing with only four calibration scores

The frozen historical fit beats exact no-request on six UNIFIL and Myanmar rows and loses on six UNAMA and emergency-session rows. Synthetic training with unusable cards improves all six sparse historical rows relative to the first fit, with losses on five dense rows. A selector uses only empty and singleton scores to choose between the two fits, fixed requests and no-request. Requiring forecast documentary quality to match no-request gives nine gains and three ties in both net and released points. It selects a learned team in three rows and a fixed request in six. The evaluation uses four exposed archives and modeled receipts. [Docket transfer, sparsity control and policy selection](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/README.md)

### Follow the qualified UNIFIL request through native receipts and the word cap

Under the strict schema, Qwen copies the China source verbatim but fills the card field with the passage. The gate rejects it and all four records score 42/70. Binding provenance to the issued source restores the opening and triggers a live Russia reply. Exact sentence assembly earns zero released points at 168 words. The compact 123-word record scores 70/70, versus 63/70 after query erasure. A fixed request for Russia yields the same native prompt, text and cost. The deliberately selected inventory is 0.2952% of the assumed prior and the all-delivered population replay gives a small net loss versus calibrated fixed routing. [Native traces, word-cap controls and fixed alternative](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/live-unifil/README.md)

### Match the public states and replace the archived record with a native writer

A fixed target can reveal whether a card already arrived when the legal gate suppresses its request. Giving the fixed responder that signal and optimizing its reply across the same private inventory eliminates all two-state wins. Three states produce one learned routing win each in UNIFIL and Myanmar under the asymmetric prior. On the archived UNIFIL bank the sum over the three equal-weight states is 196 released points for the learned route versus 189 for the strongest fixed target. Replacing the record bank with six native Qwen3.5-4B finals reverses the result to zero versus seven. The two- and three-card learned records fail the source guard by treating support for the mission as agreement with adopted provisions or with the final text. Case selection, the assumed delivery pattern and same-assistant scoring limit the comparison. [Matched audit](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/opposing-contexts/README.md) · [Native writer evidence](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/opposing-contexts/native-writer/README.md)

A source-by-source writing prompt on the same six masks raises the learned route's summed raw lower bound from 63 to 105. It still releases zero. The two-card record assigns China's sovereignty position to Brazil, and the full record is ambiguous about which delegation called for closer communication. Crediting the full record at 49 points in a guard sensitivity test gives the strongest fixed policy the same documentary gain at lower cost. This is a downstream writing bottleneck in the selected contrast. [Paired prompts, raw records, and sensitivity](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/opposing-contexts/native-writer/attributed/README.md)

### Bind final writing to one national source at a time

Four frozen Qwen3.5-4B calls wrote independently scoped public and national fragments. All eight assembled receipt masks passed the factual guard and word cap. The learned route and best fixed target each earned 105 released points across the selected three states. Fixed routing cost 0.05 less. The Russia paragraph omitted the prior compromise, leaving its card worth zero incremental rubric points. The dense route also lost on net under all three modeled inventory priors. Brazil's mission-support label supplied an exact but semantically insufficient quote, even though the separate national prose supported the label. [Source-bound composition and population checks](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/opposing-contexts/native-writer/source-bound/README.md)

An exact-excerpt variant preserved the wording of every admitted national span. The frozen writer left the China excerpt list empty, selected 27 words for Brazil under a 25-word local cap, and still omitted the prior compromise from Russia's explanation. All eight final masks passed the factual guard and released 28/70. The learned and best fixed continuations each earned 84 across the three selected states, with fixed ahead on cost. A post hoc 27-word allowance recovered Brazil's 7-point criterion and left the policy ranking unchanged. [Exact-excerpt protocol, native calls, and allowance sensitivity](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/opposing-contexts/native-writer/source-bound/extractive/README.md)

### Select source spans by ID and protect whole claims

Frozen Qwen3.5-4B selected passages from three isolated, authored UNIFIL menus. An interleaved greedy budget dropped Brazil's key qualifier from the full record. Keeping the qualified claim together and shortening the comparison restored the full 70/70 record at 150 words. The learned route beat the strongest fixed continuation 196 to 189 in the matched three-state test, with a 6.95 net margin. Passing all menu items in the given order produced 182 to 189 under the same improved packing. Across the full modeled distribution the dense route's net margins were −0.014, +0.010 and −0.169 across the three priors. The menus, public renderer and criterion mapping are authored, and transport is simulated on the exposed case. [Native selections, budget comparison, and full population replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/opposing-contexts/native-writer/source-bound/numbered/README.md)

The empty-plus-singleton forecast missed 14 points of Brazil–China complementarity. Giving the frozen dense heads one reviewed Brazil–China record recovered the numbered UNIFIL bank and raised full-population margins to +0.145, +0.012 and +0.168 over fully informed fixed controls. The analogous UK–India record repaired the archived Myanmar bank. Other pairs had no effect in either docket, and extra pair review did not help the additive UNAMA or emergency banks. These are pair-by-pair diagnostic ablations, with no rule tested for choosing a calibration pair prospectively. [Score calibration](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/opposing-contexts/native-writer/source-bound/numbered/calibration.json)

### Transfer the passage pipeline and test grading uncertainty

The Myanmar case locks UK–India for two-card calibration from the overlap criterion before native generation. The UK call returned ranked objects, which the strict parser rejected. Validating that alternate format and then preserving each full local reason raises the full score from 56 to 70. The intact record differentiates India's Council presidency from its national abstention. The matched learned route scores 196 versus 189 for optimized fixed targeting. Plain menu order also reaches those points under the same packer, so native ranking shows no documentary advantage. With the intact bank and the true pair grade, the mixed learned policy beats fully informed fixed targeting under all three modeled priors. [Native calls, case receipts, and controls](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/opposing-contexts/native-writer/source-bound/numbered/myanmar/README.md)

Across two exposed score banks, three priors and three offsets of one criterion in the pair grade, an interval-based selector reduces net losses from six to two and raises mean margin from −0.0334 to +0.0223. It preserves silence-level documentary quality in all 18 measured conditions. The two remaining losses occur on underestimated Myanmar reviews and are introduced by the interval-based selector. [Uncertainty choices and results](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/opposing-contexts/native-writer/source-bound/numbered/myanmar/robust-results.json)

### Stress the routing heads and count the grades needed for transfer

Across 360 conditions on 20 score banks, the interval selector preserved silence-level documentary quality more often than the point selector but lost more comparisons against fully informed fixed routing. A 120-update CPU fit exposed both heads to one-pair forecasting errors. The training query rate reached 99.95%, and replacing the dense heads worsened both held selectors. No case from the held matrix entered training. [Shift and fitting evidence](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/opposing-contexts/native-writer/source-bound/numbered/myanmar/shift/README.md)

An exact planner that sees the empty record, three singletons and one pair wins 31 of 48 dense synthetic conditions with clean reviews. Seeing all three pairs raises that to 48 of 48. The mixed learned route captures a smaller gain at the same budget. Under full grades and a correct prior, six apparent dense-bank losses are ties against the fixed net-optimal options that also meet the documentary floor, while seven losses persist against that filtered reference under the shifted prior. [Exact and learned calibration budgets](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/opposing-contexts/native-writer/source-bound/numbered/myanmar/shift/learned-information.json)

### Improve reward directly over the chair and responder action tables

Starting from optimized fixed targeting, one-state policy improvement wins 44 of 48 clean dense conditions at seven reviewed grades. Two-state changes recover the other four. Across 360 modeled conditions with held banks, novel inventory mixtures and grading shifts, interval-based coordinated routing beats equally informed fixed targeting in 278, loses in three, and gains 0.0743 net points on average. Fully informed fixed targeting still wins 145 comparisons. A dispatcher independently reconciles the analytical scores and message costs for 12 inventory conditions on UNIFIL and Myanmar, rebuilding all 16 cached-native records from received passage IDs. [Policy search, equal-information controls and dispatcher traces](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/opposing-contexts/native-writer/source-bound/numbered/myanmar/shift/policy/README.md)

### Transfer to the Somalia mission transition docket

Resolution 2753 has a UNBench meeting and draft join. The pinned local UNSC-Bench adopted split has no matching row, and the official UN record verifies the vote and mandate. The strict parser rejects China's numeric IDs and releases a 49/70 full record. A format-only replay reaches 70/70 at 150 words, matching the authored menu-order control. Its seven-grade interval router beats equally informed fixed routing in 15 of 18 modeled conditions, with one loss and a mean gain of 0.1176. With five grades it wins 13, with nearly the same mean gain. The 18 conditions reuse one newly evaluated docket, and model ranking adds no measured documentary quality over ordered menus. [Sources, marks, controls and dispatcher traces](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/opposing-contexts/native-writer/source-bound/numbered/somalia2753/README.md)

### Execute Somalia routing with fresh source encoders

Fresh role calls reproduced the 49-versus-42 advantage in the selected arm with a lost U.S. opening. Erasing its query or using a fixed U.S. request also scored 49. In a second selected branch hearing the query changed the reply from Somalia to the United States, but the U.S. reply omitted its reporting objection and both records scored 56. The modeled population assumes intact card content. Under that assumption query erasure costs 0.4277 net points on average. The fresh branch exposes the gap between source receipt and criterion coverage. Foreign and duplicate reply IDs were rejected without erasing their message fees. [Raw calls, role-specific prompts, faults and scores](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/opposing-contexts/native-writer/source-bound/numbered/somalia2753/live/README.md)

Grouping the U.S. vote and reporting reservation into one selectable message unit fixes the observed content omission. The fresh output wraps the selected IDs in a Markdown fence and still fails strict delivery. In the separate fence-only replay, the interval team produces a 70/70 record in 150 words, versus 56 after question erasure. A fixed U.S. target still matches it on that branch. The targeted grouping preserves the other raw model outputs and controlled delivery. [Grouped message, strict rejection and replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/opposing-contexts/native-writer/source-bound/numbered/somalia2753/live/causal/compounded/README.md)

### Separate wire validity from content and measure its routing cost

A role-local token grammar produces four valid native JSON messages on the same Somalia branch. The prefix decoder drops required China and Somalia clauses and emits an empty U.S. reply. Every arm releases 42/70. At a hypothetical 50% rate of valid addressed U.S. replies omitting the objection, frozen routing wins 4 of 18 matched-information conditions. Reoptimizing both policy families with that content risk gives the interval team 15 wins, one loss and a +0.093 mean margin. The observed branch motivates the failure type, while the stress rate remains stipulated. [Native constraints, usage and risk-aware policy replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/opposing-contexts/native-writer/source-bound/numbered/somalia2753/live/causal/compounded/structured/README.md)

Scoring whole messages over a finite predeclared set of string, integer and fenced spellings recovers the complete positions from the same four scoped prompts. The model-selected IDs are rendered as canonical JSON before transport. The interval team and fixed U.S. target each score 70/70, compared with 56 for question erasure and the equally informed interval fixed policy. Canonical-only likelihood favors empty China and U.S. messages on the same local prompts. The decoder scores 356 candidate sequences, so its inference cost is separate from the four transmitted actor requests and the modeled message fee. [Candidate scores, strict wire messages, and final records](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/evidence-reward/verified-record/historical-routing/reward-fit/family-replay/opposing-contexts/native-writer/source-bound/numbered/somalia2753/live/causal/compounded/structured/whole_message/README.md)

### Transfer the decoder and routing policy to Resolution 2422

Three frozen Qwen3.5-4B prompts on the v1.4 Tribunals case selected all six authored role clauses using 78 scored wire sequences. The final record releases 70/70 in 131 words, matching the authored menu in every receipt mask. Ranking only the canonical spelling on the same prompts leaves two clauses and releases 52/70. Only the live model calls and their selections were measured on hardware. The chair renders authored clauses, and delivery and private inventories are modeled.

Frozen Myanmar and UNIFIL routers beat their same-information fixed controls in 10 of 12 conditions, lose twice and average +0.085 net points. Replanning from seven Tribunals grades with the same openings wins 32 of 36 and ties four, averaging +0.128. The 36 rows collapse to 21 distinct settings and one documentary loss to informed silence. A wider prior envelope preserves the same failing selection because its candidate pool contains no policy that meets the floor throughout those forecasts. This establishes controlled routing transfer on one exposed case, with independent grading and free-form deliberation still unmeasured. [Live calls, exact rubric marks, policy tables and replays](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/tribunals-v14-transfer/README.md)

### Meet the documentary floor with local responder randomization

The Resolution 2422 failure survives every legal deterministic policy. An exhaustive search of 864 chair maps finds that 44 can meet the nine forecast floors if the responder can randomize, and none of the four fixed-target maps can. The selected adaptive policy randomizes a single local reply. It raises held quality from 0.059 points below informed silence to 0.019 above it at the same packet cost. Its held net is 0.007 higher than fully informed fixed routing, while both randomized fixed controls remain below silence in held quality.

Across the 21 distinct conditions on this exposed case, using the expanded search only for a policy family with an empty qualification pool removes the sole held quality loss. The updated team wins 19 and ties two against the stronger of the archived and updated fixed controls in each condition. It wins 17 and loses four against fully informed fixed routing. These are modeled channel and score conditions on the same source bank. [Optimization, stochastic runtime and all replays](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/tribunals-v14-transfer/constrained/README.md)

### Test the stochastic repair across archived UN cases

Across 90 modeled conditions on five previously scored UN banks, the conditional stochastic router beats the stronger matched fixed comparator in 79, ties nine and loses two. Exact deterministic expansion has the same record and slightly higher mean net. Of 26 adaptive searches triggered by an empty candidate pool, only one needs randomization to meet the forecasts. In that Myanmar setting the held net falls 0.156 points. Twenty-three expanded searches find the quality floor infeasible. Minimizing the worst forecast shortfall in those rows raises mean held quality by 0.014 points but lowers net by 0.012 and increases the fall-below-silence count from 10 to 11. Substituting the paired exact grade for the contested pair removes all 10 original regressions in an archived counterfactual, gaining 0.046 net points per triggered review before review cost. The cases are exposed. The repeat-review diagnostic assumes exact correction and does not measure live judge reliability. [Cross-case policy searches and paired review replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/tribunals-v14-transfer/constrained/transfer/README.md)

### Reread contested routing grades with a live judge

Six blinded Qwen3.5-4B calls on the Emergency Session, Myanmar and UNAMA pair records recover zero of the three archived rubric grades in either format. The numeric and quote-grounded Emergency Session grades and the numeric UNAMA grade are valid but incorrectly return the protected public base of 34 instead of 46. The UNAMA quote verdict imports absent Russian statements from the source. The Myanmar numeric score is outside the rubric lattice and the capped quoted prefix credits absent procedural text. In the 23 archived trigger conditions, accepting the valid but wrong grades reduces the original 10 below-silence outcomes to two in the numeric arm and four in the quote-grounded arm. Returning the protected base on the same rows would have the same routing effect. Exact review removes all ten in the counterfactual. Judge accuracy, quote validity and routing utility remain separate measurements. [Live replies, token audit and routing replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/tribunals-v14-transfer/constrained/transfer/live-review/README.md)

### Separate candidate coverage from source support

Twelve Qwen3.5-4B calls on three disputed records and three controls separate the candidate-only rubric pass from the source-verification pass. Verification matches all 21 paragraph labels, including the false current veto. The coverage pass recovers no exact disputed grade. On the Emergency Session pair it credits a national position from the public-vote paragraph, releasing 50 against an archived 46. Myanmar and UNAMA pair replies are malformed. In the 23 triggered routing rows, admitting the one overgrade increases below-silence outcomes from 10 to 16. A separate replay checks that cited paragraphs carry the owning source tag already emitted by the modular renderer. It recovers 46 on the Emergency Session pair and reduces below-silence outcomes to four. It leaves both malformed records unchanged. The structured repair is retrospective and the cases are exposed. [Prompts, controls, raw judgments and routing results](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/tribunals-v14-transfer/constrained/transfer/live-review/separated/README.md)

### Constrain coverage judgments to legal criterion labels

Constrained next-token scoring returns legal outputs in all 106 Qwen3.5-4B invocations. The source-scoped agreement rule recovers the UNAMA pair and public-only control and abstains on the other four records. Averaging the two mapped logits recovers both modular pairs but falsely awards the Myanmar chair criterion, raising that pair to 70. Applied retrospectively only to tagged inputs, the average yields four admitted exact grades and leaves the two untagged Myanmar records unresolved. That selective policy improves eight of 23 triggered routing rows with no net loss and reduces below-silence outcomes from 10 to two. This is a diagnostic on the existing exposed records with archived source guards. [Locked prompts, native logits, and routing replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/tribunals-v14-transfer/constrained/transfer/live-review/separated/atomic/README.md)

### Test procedural capacity with matched coverage contrasts

On the Myanmar case, factoring the presidency criterion into a procedural announcement and a distinct national explanation improves exact coverage from four of seven to seven of seven when averaging the two answer-letter mappings. Factoring the UK criterion recovers the two archival positives but falsely credits both controls that omit a required part. The factored judge admits the disputed 63-point Myanmar grade, and with the earlier source-scoped grades it reproduces the exact-review routing replay. Across 23 modeled conditions, ten improve over the initial grades, none lose net, and zero remain below informed silence. The results depend on the three exposed case records and archived source guards. They distinguish a role-specific repair from a general benefit of decomposition. [Matched controls, direct logits and exact replay](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/tribunals-v14-transfer/constrained/transfer/live-review/separated/atomic/capacity/README.md)

### Fit cooperating policies from shared documentary returns

A paired-rollout fit trains chair and responder action-value residuals without planner labels. On 90 held scenarios over five archival UN banks, it beats the matched fixed policy in 52 and loses to its own neutral construction in 86. A component swap attributes most of the extra cost to the fitted responder. A separate learned reward forecast restricts changes to two uncertain score entries. Synthetic validation selects half strength. The selected policy beats matched fixed in 69 of 90, gains 0.0052 net points on average over neutral, and lowers below-silence outcomes from 15 to 10. Exact forecast planning remains stronger on mean net. Across 23 scenarios with live-verified pair grades, the earlier robust reviewed planner delivers zero below-silence outcomes. Replacing its inferred triple score with the fitted forecast yields the same mean release and loses 0.0040 net on average. These CPU fits use simulated shared returns and exposed held case banks. [Weights, component ablation and dispatch replays](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/cooperative-rollout-fit/README.md)

### Transfer to the tolerance and international peace docket

Resolution 2686 is a prospective issue-family transfer with a verified UNBench author-test event and official vote. The pinned UNSC-Bench adopted split lacks the case. Three frozen Qwen notes are reused for eight free-form receipt records, a reversed full receipt and a pooled-source writer. France plus Switzerland reaches 35/70. Adding the UK note produces a 190-word output, failing the 175-word cap. Reversal conflates the ICCPR omission with a separate Swiss objection on women's rights proposals. The pooled writer reaches 21/70. The robust router beats matched fixed in eight of 18 simulations on the prospective bank and ties ten. A separately authored 174-word assembly preserves the original notes and scores 63/70. The robust router wins all 18 matched fixed comparisons on that retrospective assembly bank. Forecast fitting adds zero to the prospective free-form bank. These results separate native synthesis from the value of adaptive delivery on a verifiable record. [Prospective lock, native outputs, rubric marks and dispatch](research/unsc-marl-20261119/benchmark-v1-runs/tolerance-2686-transfer/README.md)

With the original adaptive router held fixed, changing only the writer reduces expected released points from 22.65 to 12.51 and turns a +0.0558 mean margin over matched fixed into −0.0040. Replanning on the revised bank delivers more UK notes and yields 12 wins and six losses with a −0.1233 mean margin. The learned forecast correction loses to neutral in 17 of 18 rows. Frozen Qwen3.5-4B writes a public lead and three isolated delegation rewrites under the original combined output ceiling. Using the same generated lead and the verbatim original notes restores France's alternative-forum argument and releases 63/70 at exactly 175 words. That one-call variant matches the eight grades from the earlier authored public lead. ### Transfer learned chair and responder policies onto both generated writer banks

The frozen fitted responder accounts for the joint team's loss on the stronger Resolution 2686 writer. The fitted team loses 16 of 18 against zero residuals and all 18 against robust adaptive planning and fully informed fixed routing. Synthetic validation of a fresh fit selects a half-strength chair and zero responder residual. It comes close to neutral on 90 historical conditions but still loses all 18 robust comparisons on the verbatim writer. The frozen learned reward forecast gains +0.0268 mean net against robust search on the scoped-rewrite bank, winning six and losing 12 with zero below-silence outcomes. On the verbatim bank it loses all 18 robust comparisons. Exact adaptive search on the announced forecast comes within 0.0045 mean net of robust routing on that bank.
